### [1] [The incremental voter model: mean-field analysis and convergence to equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28984)
*Fei Cao,Xiaoqian Gong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了增量选民模型（IVM），这是一个离散意见多智能体系统，通过平均场极限分析研究了意见分布的渐进行为，为社会影响过程中的意见极化建模提供了新的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的选民模型通常假设意见是二值的，难以捕捉现实社会中意见演化的渐变特性。为了更真实地建模社会影响过程，特别是意见极化现象，需要开发一种允许意见逐步变化的模型，并建立严格的数学框架来分析其长期行为。

Method: 提出增量选民模型（IVM）：智能体持有离散集合{-k,...,0,...,k}中的意见；每步随机选取一对智能体（听众和说服者），听众根据说服者的意见更新自身意见，每次最多改变一个单位。通过推导控制大群体极限的非线性常微分方程组（ODEs），建立了研究意见分布在平均场极限下渐近行为的严格数学框架。

Result: 成功推导出了控制大群体智能体模型的平均场非线性常微分方程组，为分析意见分布的渐近行为提供了严格的数学框架。该模型能够捕捉意见的逐步演化特性，为理解复杂系统中的意见极化现象提供了新的理论工具。

Conclusion: 本研究为社会影响过程的理解做出了贡献，特别是在意见极化建模方面提供了新的视角和方法。该框架可以为未来更先进模型的制定提供指导，有助于深入理解复杂社会系统中的意见动态演化机制。

Abstract: We introduce the incremental voter model (IVM), a discrete-opinion multi-agent system where agents undergo step-wise transitions biased by the opinion of a randomly selected persuader. Our incremental voter model comprises a large population of interacting agents, each holding an opinion represented by an element of the discrete set $\{-k,\ldots,0,\ldots,k\}, k \in \mathbb{N}_{+}$. At each update step as time progresses, a pair of distinct agents are selected independently and uniformly at random from the population, and the first agent (viewed as the ``listener'') updates its opinion based on that of the second (viewed as the ``persuader''), adopting a new opinion that differs from its current one by at most one unit. By deriving the mean-field system of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) that governs the large-population limit of the agent-based model, we develop a rigorous mathematical framework to study the asymptotic behavior of the opinion distribution in the mean-field limit. These results contribute to a deeper understanding of social influence processes in complex systems, particularly in modeling opinion polarization, and may guide the formulation of more advanced models in future research.

</details>


### [2] [LLM-ALSO: LLM-Driven Adaptive Learning-Signal Optimization for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29293)
*Xiaoguang Wu,Zhi Zheng,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM-ALSO框架，通过迭代式的诊断、提议和验证流程，利用大语言模型自适应地生成学习信号，有效解决了稀疏奖励下多智能体强化学习的训练指导问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励的多智能体强化学习（MARL）中，有效训练指导是一个核心挑战。弱监督限制了协调与策略改进，而现有方法往往需要大量领域专业知识或手动设计工作。虽然大语言模型为灵活的学习信号设计提供了新的可能，但现有LLM方法主要面向单智能体、一次性操作，或在协作MARL的动态训练过程中验证不足。

Method: 提出了LLM-ALSO框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Critic LLM：从稀疏回报指标和紧凑行为证据中诊断阶段特定的学习和协调失败；(2) Generator LLM：根据诊断结果提出候选奖励塑形配置；(3) 分支验证反馈：在影响主训练轨迹之前对候选方案进行细化。该方法采用短期验证和阶段感知的自适应策略，确保只有经过验证的更新才会被引入训练过程。

Result: 在稀疏奖励协作MARL任务上的实验表明，LLM-ALSO能够提升稀疏评估性能和学习效率，证明了该方法在稀疏奖励环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM-ALSO成功地将大语言模型引入多智能体强化学习的学习信号优化过程，通过迭代式的诊断-提议-验证机制，有效降低了不可靠LLM生成修改的风险，为稀疏奖励下的MARL训练指导提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Effective training-time guidance is central to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), yet remains difficult in sparse-reward settings where weak supervision limits coordination and policy improvement, and existing methods often require substantial domain expertise or manual design effort. Large language models (LLMs) provide a promising alternative for flexible learning-signal design, yet existing LLM-based methods remain largely single-agent-oriented, one-shot, or weakly validated for the evolving training dynamics of cooperative MARL. To address these limitations, we propose LLM-ALSO, an iterative LLM-driven adaptive learning-signal optimization framework for MARL. Rather than directly deploying LLM-generated rewards, LLM-ALSO decomposes adaptation into iterative diagnosis, proposal, and validation: a Critic LLM diagnoses stage-specific learning and coordination failures from sparse-return metrics and compact behavior evidence, a Generator LLM proposes candidate reward-shaping configurations conditioned on the diagnosis, and branch-validation feedback refines candidates before they affect the main training trajectory. Through short-horizon validation and stage-aware adaptation, LLM-ALSO promotes only validated updates into training, reducing the risk of unreliable LLM-generated modifications. Experiments on sparse-reward cooperative MARL tasks show that LLM-ALSO improves sparse-evaluation performance and learning efficiency.

</details>


### [3] [DynaGraph: Lightweight Multi-Model Interaction Framework via Dynamic Topological Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29511)
*Yanxing Guo,Zihao Zheng,Fangzhou Wu,Ling Liang,Lin Bao,Zongwei Wang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出DynaGraph框架，通过动态拓扑重配置和PEFT适配器复用，使8B模型在单张消费级GPU上逼近72B单体的推理能力，同时大幅降低延迟和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模单体LLM存在严重的计算冗余；现有的结构化流水线面临预定义静态拓扑对级联错误脆弱、无约束动态智能体存在轨迹发散和内存膨胀的两难困境。

Method: 在执行层，于共享基座模型上复用时分PEFT适配器；在路由层，评估器持续监控执行置信度，触发分级自愈机制：细粒度修补应对局部数据缺口，子图重构应对严重逻辑断裂。

Result: 在StrategyQA、MATH和FinQA上，8B模型性能逼近72B单体（如StrategyQA 87.6%、MATH 82.7%）；相比无约束动态架构，延迟降低68.1%，token消耗减少68.6%。

Conclusion: DynaGraph证明了轻量级多模型框架通过动态拓扑重配置能有效平衡推理能力与资源效率，为复杂推理任务提供了高效可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Tackling complex reasoning tasks typically relies on massive monolithic LLMs, which suffer from severe computational redundancy. While task decomposition through structured pipelines or multi-agent collaborations offers an alternative, these approaches inevitably fall into a critical dilemma: predefined static topologies are highly vulnerable to cascading errors, whereas unconstrained dynamic agents suffer from trajectory divergence and unpredictable memory bloat. To address this, we present DynaGraph, a lightweight multi-model framework driven by dynamic topological reconfiguration. At the execution level, DynaGraph multiplexes time-division PEFT adapters over a shared base model, enabling both full system training and inference deployment on a single consumer-grade GPU. At the routing level, the Evaluator continuously monitors execution confidence to trigger hierarchical self-healing: Fine-grained Patching for localized data gaps and Subgraph Reconstruction for severe logical ruptures. Experiments on StrategyQA, MATH, and FinQA demonstrate our 8B model closely approximates the reasoning capabilities of a 72B monolithic model (e.g., 87.6% on StrategyQA, 82.7% on MATH). Furthermore, it reduces latency by up to 68.1% and token consumption by 68.6% compared to unconstrained dynamic architectures.

</details>


### [4] [CONCAT: Consensus- and Confidence-Driven Ad Hoc Teaming for Efficient LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29612)
*Ziyang Ma,Dingyi Zhang,Sichu Liang,Jiajia Chu,Pengfei Xia,Hui Zang,Deyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CONCAT，一种无需训练的多智能体协作框架，通过共识聚类、置信度选举和心理理论预测协作收益来组织智能体网络，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低了通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统虽然性能优于单智能体，但智能体间频繁通信导致巨大的计算开销。以往通过训练稀疏图或微调规划器的方法虽能缓解问题，但引入了额外训练成本且限制了系统的泛化能力。

Method: 提出CONCAT框架：1）基于初始答案对智能体进行聚类；2）根据置信度选择各聚类领导者；3）设计基于心理理论的启发式函数，根据答案和置信度预测领导者间的协作收益；4）根据预测收益剔除部分通信，动态构建ad hoc多智能体网络。整个过程无需任务特定训练。

Result: 在三个LLM和三个基准测试上，CONCAT相比LLM-Debate实现了最高2.02倍的效率提升（准确率/延迟比），在Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct上平均延迟降低50.1%，且性能优于AgentDropout等需要训练的方法。

Conclusion: CONCAT证明了无需训练的多智能体协作策略能够有效平衡性能与效率，避免了训练方法的成本和泛化性限制，为大语言模型多智能体系统的高效部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Although large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems (MAS) show their capability to solve complex tasks and achieve higher performance over single agent systems, they lead to huge computational overheads because of heavy communication between agents. Previous research has made efforts to train a sparse multi-agent graph or fine-tune a planner to orchestrate the workflow better. However, such extra training processes introduce computational costs and limit MAS to specific domains, therefore compromising their generalizability. In this paper, we propose CONCAT, a training-free multi-agent collaboration framework based on CONsensus and Confidence-driven Ad hoc Teaming to efficiently organize agent interactions. Specifically, agents are clustered based on their initial answers, and leaders of each cluster are selected based on the agents' confidence. Then, a heuristic function based on the Theory of Mind is designed to predict the collaboration benefits between every two leaders according to their answers and confidence. Finally, an ad hoc multi-agent network is organized after evicting a percentage of communications based on the predicted benefits. Experiments across three LLMs and three benchmarks show that CONCAT achieves up to 2.02x higher efficiency (accuracy/latency ratio) than LLM-Debate and outperforms training-aware methods such as AgentDropout, while reducing average latency by 50.1% on Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, without any task-specific training.

</details>


### [5] [Evolve as a Team: Collaborative Self-Evolution for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29790)
*Zhezheng Hao,Tianfu Wang,Huanshuo Dong,Ziyan Liu,Hong Wang,Xiankun Lin,Qiang Lin,Can Wang,Hande Dong,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Meta-Team框架，通过协作自演化实现基于经验的多智能体系统演化，在长期任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中基于大语言模型的多智能体系统(MAS)在执行过程中常出现各种失败，且这些失败难以在设计阶段完全消除，促使研究者探索基于执行经验驱动的MAS演化方法。然而，MAS经验具有长期性和复杂性，多个智能体的执行链和通信消息交织在一起，难以识别具体需要改进的内容。

Method: 提出Meta-Team框架，包含两个核心设计：1)保留每个智能体的执行上下文并协调任务后通信，使智能体能够交换分布式演化证据；2)进行多尺度自演化，将执行经验转化为对智能体行为、智能体间协调和团队级组织的可重用改进。

Result: 在六个长期智能体基准测试中，Meta-Team始终优于单智能体系统、手工设计的MAS和先前的MAS演化方法；进一步分析表明Meta-Team实现了更可靠和可扩展的MAS自演化。

Conclusion: Meta-Team成功实现了基于经验驱动的多智能体系统自演化，为复杂长期任务中的MAS可靠性提升提供了有效解决方案，展现出优越的性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as an effective paradigm for complex and long-horizon tasks. However, in real-world tasks, MAS often exhibit various failures during execution and such failures are difficult to eliminate during design. This motivates experience-driven MAS evolution, where a system improves based on its own execution experience. Yet such evolution is challenging because MAS experience is prolonged and intricate, interleaving multiple agents' execution chains and communication messages, which makes it difficult to identify what should be improved. To address this challenge, we propose Meta-Team, an experience-driven MAS evolution framework based on collaborative self-evolution. Meta-Team preserves the execution context of each agent and coordinates post-task communication, enabling agents to exchange distributed evidence for evolution. Building on this design, Meta-Team conducts multi-scale self-evolution, transforming execution experience into reusable improvements to agent behaviors, inter-agent coordination, and team-level organization. Across six long-horizon agent benchmarks, Meta-Team consistently outperforms single-agent systems, hand-crafted MAS, and prior MAS evolution methods; further analyses demonstrate that Meta-Team enables more reliable and scalable MAS self-evolution.

</details>


### [6] [Evolutionary Dynamics of Cooperation in Next-Generation LLM Agent Systems: A Cross-Provider Empirical Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29874)
*Francisco León Zúñiga Bolívar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究将合作偏见基准测试扩展至2025-2026年的四款前沿LLM，发现合作偏见在下一代模型中持续存在，但跨提供商差异显著；提供商身份而非模型代际是均衡结果的最强预测因子，噪声仍是普遍挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究下一代LLM智能体是否继承了其前代模型中记录的合作偏见，抑或规模扩大和提供商多样性会重塑竞争性多智能体设置中的均衡行为。

Method: 将Willis等人建立的演化博弈论和迭代囚徒困境（IPD）基准协议扩展至四款前沿模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.4 Mini），在三种提示风格（Default、Prose、Self-Refine）和四种群体组成（平衡与有偏，含噪与不含噪）条件下进行测试，每种条件进行500次Moran迭代。

Result: (1)合作偏见跨提供商持续存在（H1）：12种模型-提示组合中有9种在平衡无噪条件下偏向合作均衡；(2)跨提供商差异显著（H3）：Gemini 2.5 Flash在偏态条件下高达77%的激进均衡，而GPT-5.4 Mini在Self-Refine下达到70%的合作均衡；(3)对激进能力对等的支持有限（H2）：Self-Refine提高了所有模型的ICD，Claude Sonnet 4.6 Refine达到数据集中最高的ICD（0.913），但Default和Prose提示未显示系统性收窄；(4)噪声鲁棒性证据方向性积极但不稳健（H4）：Claude Sonnet 4.6的平均噪声敏感度约6个百分点，而Claude 3.5 Sonnet为13个百分点，但该跨研究差距在传播前代未报告的采样误差后不具统计显著性。

Conclusion: 提供商身份而非模型代际是均衡结果的最强关联因素；噪声仍然是跨越模型规模或年代的普遍挑战；合作偏见在下一代模型中持续存在，但表现程度因提供商而异。

Abstract: Do next-generation LLM agents inherit the cooperative biases documented in their predecessors, or does scale and provider diversity reshape equilibrium behaviour in competitive multi-agent settings? Willis et al. established a benchmark for this question using evolutionary game theory and the Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD), finding consistent cooperative biases in ChatGPT-4o and Claude 3.5 Sonnet. We extend this benchmark to four frontier models released in 2025-2026 - Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.4 Mini - applying the identical protocol across three prompting styles (Default, Prose, Self-Refine) and four population compositions (balanced and biased, with and without noise). Cooperative bias persists across providers (H1): nine of twelve model-prompt combinations favour cooperative equilibria in balanced noiseless conditions. Cross-provider divergence is substantial (H3): Gemini 2.5 Flash reaches up to 77% aggressive equilibria under biased conditions, while GPT-5.4 Mini reaches 70% cooperative equilibria under Self-Refine. Support for aggressive capability parity is partial (H2): Self-Refine raises ICD in all models and Claude Sonnet 4.6 Refine achieves the highest ICD in the dataset (0.913), but Default and Prose prompts show no systematic narrowing. Evidence on noise robustness is directionally positive but not robustly confirmed (H4): with n=500 Moran iterations per condition, average noise sensitivity is approximately 6 percentage points for Claude Sonnet 4.6 versus 13 pp for Claude 3.5 Sonnet, but this cross-study gap is not statistically significant once the predecessor's unreported sampling error is propagated. Provider identity, rather than model generation, is the strongest correlate of equilibrium outcomes; noise remains a universal challenge regardless of model size or vintage.

</details>


### [7] [Discovering Cooperative Pipelines: Autoresearch for Sequential Social Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30003)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了双层自动研究框架，外层AI代理自主重新设计内层LLM策略合成系统的流程，用于解决多智能体序列社会困境问题。实验表明，研究代理优于手工设计的基线，并发现了目标依赖的流程设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索AI系统能否自主地进行研究工作，特别是在多智能体合作领域。传统方法依赖人工设计流程，而本研究希望让AI代理自动优化LLM策略合成系统的流程，包括系统提示、反馈函数和迭代逻辑等，从而更好地解决序列社会困境中的合作问题。

Method: 采用双层自动研究框架：外层研究者代理$\mathcal{R}$作为编码代理，读取内层源代码，编辑系统提示、反馈函数、辅助库和迭代逻辑，运行评估并决定保留哪些改进。实验在两个游戏（Cleanup和Gathering）上进行，使用两个策略合成LLM，测试两种福利目标（功利主义效率和罗尔斯主义最大最小值）。

Result: 研究者代理在所有实验条件下均可靠地超越了手工设计的基线，显著降低了运行间的方差，并优于仅进行提示优化的方法。更重要的是，发现的流程具有目标依赖性：只有在最大化最小值目标下，研究者才会在合成器流程中注入显式的公平机制，这类机制在目标无关的系统提示和所有效率优化的流程中都不存在。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持信息设计视角，即研究者代理根据福利目标选择向有限理性的合成器揭示什么信息。这表明AI系统能够自主发现并实现目标特定的解决方案，特别是在公平性方面展现出自动设计能力，为AI自动化研究提供了新的可能性和见解。

Abstract: We study two-level autoresearch for cooperation: an outer-loop AI agent autonomously redesigns the inner-loop pipeline of an LLM policy-synthesis system for multi-agent Sequential Social Dilemmas (SSDs). A researcher agent $\mathcal{R}$ (run as a coding agent) reads the inner-loop source code, edits system prompts, feedback functions, helper libraries, and iteration logic, runs evaluations, and decides what to keep, following the autoresearch paradigm. Across two games (Cleanup and Gathering), two policy-synthesizer LLMs, and two welfare objectives (utilitarian efficiency and Rawlsian maximin), the researcher reliably exceeds hand-designed baselines, sharply tightens run-to-run variance, and outperforms prompt-only optimization. The discovered pipelines are objective-dependent: only under maximin does the researcher inject an explicit fairness mechanism into synthesizer pipelines, a class of mechanism that is absent from its own objective-agnostic system prompt and from every efficiency-optimized pipeline. This supports an information-design reading in which the researcher chooses what to reveal to the boundedly rational synthesizer as a function of the welfare objective. Code at https://github.com/vicgalle/autoresearch-social-dilemmas.

</details>


### [8] [When Cloud Agents Meet Device Agents: Lessons from Hybrid Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30102)
*Corrado Rainone,Davide Belli,Bence Major,Arash Behboodi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了结合设备端小模型与云端大模型的混合多智能体系统设计，揭示了在性能、成本和功耗间的权衡关系，发现最优架构高度依赖任务特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合多智能体系统缺乏通用设计原则，组件选择通常基于特定领域的临时决策。需要在任务准确率、货币成本和边缘能耗之间寻找平衡点，系统化探索混合推理的设计空间。

Method: 将两种代表性的多智能体系统架构改造为支持混合推理，系统性地研究各项设计选择如何改变系统在功耗、成本和性能帕累托前沿上的运行点。

Result: 研究发现：小语言模型能够有效借助大语言模型的辅助；但最优架构高度依赖任务特性；更多前沿级计算能力并不一定带来更好的性能表现。混合MAS设计呈现出复杂微妙的图景。

Conclusion: 混合多智能体系统设计没有放之四海而皆准的方案，需要根据具体任务特点进行针对性设计。单纯增加计算资源并不能保证性能提升，设计者需要在性能、成本和能耗之间进行仔细权衡。

Abstract: The design space of agentic AI inference spans two extremes: frontier large language models (LLMs), typically hosted in the cloud and offering strong performance across a wide range of tasks at substantially high cost, and more cost-efficient small language models (SLMs), which are amenable to on-device inference. Hybrid multi-agent systems (MASs) combining on-device and cloud models offer a promising middle ground, but they also introduce a complex and poorly understood design space in which task accuracy, monetary cost, and edge energy consumption are tightly coupled; in the absence of general design principles, hybrid components, although not the most prevalent choice, are typically introduced through ad hoc decisions tailored to specific domains. In this work, we examine this design space more systematically. We adapt two representative MAS architectures to support hybrid inference and study how individual design choices shift the operating point along the Pareto frontier of power, cost, and performance. Our findings paint a nuanced picture of hybrid MAS design: while SLMs can effectively benefit from LLM assistance, the optimal architecture is highly task-dependent, and greater frontier-level compute does not consistently translate to better performance.

</details>


### [9] [Unifying Temporal and Structural Credit Assignment in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30227)
*Wenwu Li,Yuran Song,Mingze Zhao,Bo Jin,Wenhao Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体系统（MAS）优化困难的问题，提出了一种基于时序和结构信用分配的离散块坐标下降算法，通过分解目标函数、精准定位薄弱环节，在降低查询复杂度的同时显著提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统优化方法面临核心挑战：计算图的离散和不可微特性导致传统梯度方法失效，全局监督信号稀疏使得难以将轨迹级别的失败归因到具体组件，黑盒优化器因此只能进行低效且高方差的探索。作者认为需要引入结构归纳偏置来解耦错误信号，从而实现可处理的MAS优化。

Method: 提出时序和结构信用分配机制，将目标沿两个轴分解：(i)时序信用：利用状态空间瓶颈识别关键轮次；(ii)结构信用：使用稳态角色策略隔离各智能体的贡献。基于分解信号，设计了离散化、语言化的块坐标下降算法进行迭代优化，交替优化角色提示和聚合协议，利用LLM生成的"代理梯度"仅针对识别出的薄弱环节进行定向更新。

Result: 在多种推理基准测试中，该方法显著降低了查询复杂度，同时提升了系统性能，证明了其在优化MAS方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为自改进型MAS提供了一条有原则且可解释的优化路径，证明了通过结构性信用分配和定向更新可以有效解决MAS优化中的关键挑战。

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) empower Large Language Models to tackle complex reasoning tasks through collaborative interaction, optimizing their dynamics remains a formidable challenge due to the discrete, non-differentiable nature of the computation graph and the sparsity of global supervisory signals. Existing black-box optimizers struggle to attribute trajectory-level failure to specific local components, resulting in inefficient, high-variance exploration. We argue that tractable MAS optimization needs structural inductive biases to disentangle error signals. We propose temporal and structural credit assignment, which decomposes the objective along two axes: (i) temporal credit, using state-space bottlenecks to identify critical rounds, and (ii) structural credit, using stationary role policies to isolate agent contributions. Leveraging these decomposed signals, we introduce a discrete, verbalized block coordinate descent algorithm for iterative refinement. Rather than indiscriminate global updates, it alternates between optimizing role prompts and aggregation protocols, using LLM-generated "proxy gradients" to target only the identified weak links. Across diverse reasoning benchmarks, our approach substantially reduces query complexity while improving performance, providing a principled and interpretable path toward self-improving MAS.

</details>

*Sneheel Sarangi,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Aurélien Bück-Kaeffer,Zachary Yang,Jean-François Godbout,Reihaneh Rabbany*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了EASE框架（环境、智能体、模拟引擎、评估指标）用于标准化LLM多智能体模拟，并开源实现了SiliSocS硅社会沙盒系统，通过三个案例研究验证了框架在提高研究可复现性和评估设计选择影响方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM社会交互模拟器多为临时构建且架构单一，缺乏架构标准化导致研究难以复现且下游评估复杂，阻碍了基于LLM的多智能体模拟科学研究的严谨发展。

Method: 提出EASE框架将核心组件模块化为环境、智能体、模拟引擎和评估指标四个部分；将其封装在实验研究模式中以编排围绕显式研究问题的工作流；开发开源的SiliSocS硅社会沙盒系统实现研究就绪的EASE配置。

Result: 通过三个案例研究展示了系统的能力：对现有问题的全面评估、深入探究复杂问题、以及对现有研究的扩展；揭示了当前建模方法的局限性，并隔离了设计选择对关键结果的影响。

Conclusion: EASE框架和SiliSocS系统为LLM社会模拟研究提供了标准化架构和可复现的实验平台，有助于推动该领域向更加严谨的科学方向发展，并为研究者提供了深入分析和评估设计选择的有效工具。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed to simulate social interactions, yet many of the existing simulators remain ad hoc and monolithic. This lack of architectural standardization prevents reproducible research and complicates downstream evaluation. We advance a rigorous science of LLM-based multi-agent simulation by modularizing core components into Environments, Agents, Simulation engines, and Evaluation metrics (EASE). We demonstrate the utility of EASE configuration by wrapping it in an experimental study schema for orchestrating workflows centered around answering explicit research questions in generated scenarios. We contribute SiliSocS, an open-source, research-ready Silicon Society Sandbox implementing a study-structured EASE configuration to enable highly configurable and reproducible LLM-based social simulations. Using SiliSocS and EASE, we present three case studies, showcasing the system's comprehensive assessment of existing questions, ability to dive deeper into complex questions, and elaboration of existing studies, respectively. Together, these case studies highlight the limitations of current modeling approaches and isolate the impacts of design choices on key results.

</details>


### [11] [SpecBench: Evaluating Specification-Level Reasoning for Software Engineering LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30314)
*Grant Hamblin,Kevin Song,Zhanda Zhu,Anand Jayarajan,Sihang Liu,Nandita Vijaykumar,Gennady Pekhimenko*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpecBench，首个评估软件工程Agent在规格设计阶段推理能力的基准，基于开源项目的RFC流程构建任务，测试Agent识别初始设计方案缺陷的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如SWE-Bench）聚焦于代码生成阶段，假设规格说明已正确且完整。但在真实复杂软件系统中，初始规格往往不完整且有缺陷，需要经过专家审查和修订。缺乏评估Agent在规格设计阶段推理能力的基准。

Method: 基于成熟开源项目的RFC（Request for Comments）流程构建任务。每个任务提供：初始设计提案、项目代码库、历史RFC讨论记录。Agent需要识别初始提案中的规格缺陷（遗漏、歧义、不一致或错误假设）。评估基于历史RFC审查中专家维护者提出的真实批评意见。涵盖5个仓库：Kubernetes、React、Rust、TVM和vLLM。

Result: 评估了最先进的SWE agents在SpecBench上的表现。最佳模型GPT-5.4仅达到44.4%的准确率，表明现有Agent在无执行反馈的情况下进行系统设计推理的能力仍有很大提升空间。

Conclusion: SpecBench填补了评估软件工程生命周期早期阶段能力的空白，揭示了当前SWE agents在规格级推理方面的局限性，为未来改进方向提供了重要的基准和洞察。

Abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents are transitioning from code generation to full software development lifecycle automation. A critical phase in this lifecycle is specification design: transforming initial proposals into carefully considered requirements through expert review. Existing benchmarks such as SWE-Bench are implementation-focused by measuring the agent's ability to generate code given fixed, precise design requirements. This formulation assumes specifications are correct and complete. In real-world complex and critical software systems, initial specifications are often incomplete and flawed, requiring extensive expert reviews and revisions before being accepted for implementation. To fill this gap, we introduce SpecBench to evaluate specification-level reasoning: the ability to generate complete, unambiguous, consistent, and correct system specifications. SpecBench tasks are derived from the Request for Comments (RFC) process used by mature open-source projects. For each task, an agent is given an initial design proposal, the project codebase, and all past project RFC discussions. The agent is tasked with identifying specification deficiencies: omissions, ambiguities, inconsistencies, or incorrect assumptions in the initial proposal. We evaluate predictions against critiques raised by expert maintainers during historical RFC reviews. SpecBench contains tasks from 5 diverse repositories: Kubernetes, React, Rust, TVM, and vLLM. We evaluate state-of-the-art SWE agents on SpecBench, analyzing their capacity to reason about system design without execution feedback. The best performing agent, GPT-5.4, achieves 44.4% accuracy.

</details>

### [12] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了STHTD-MP方法，通过使用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替代特征协方差矩阵作为度量，改进了Mirror-Prox TD方法的更新几何结构，理论证明其在特定条件下具有更小的平均收缩因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度时序差分方法在实际性能上严重受辅助变量度量诱导的几何结构影响。传统的Mirror-Prox TD方法使用特征协方差度量，而混合TD方法表明行为策略转移信息可以提供更有信息的更新几何结构。这促使研究者探索如何更好地利用行为策略信息来改进Mirror-Prox方法的性能。

Method: 提出了行为策略诱导的Mirror-Prox时序差分方法(STHTD-MP)。该方法在原始-对偶鞍点公式中，用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替代协方差度量。保持原始变量和辅助变量的单一学习率，并对混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤。在标准随机逼近假设下进行理论分析，包括正定性验证、Hurwitz条件、Lyapunov有界性论证和ODE收敛性分析。

Result: 推导了投影-预言机遍历间隙界限和基于确定性Mirror-Prox误差矩阵谱半径的GTD2-MP精确平均算子比较。分析表明当行为策略诱导度量改善鞍点几何结构时，STHTD-MP可以比GTD2-MP具有更小的平均收缩因子。在二状态、Random Walk和Boyan Chain基准测试上的精确数值平均算子分析支持了这一结论，而Baird反例被识别为严格假设失效的奇异边界情况。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP方法通过引入行为策略信息成功改进了Mirror-Prox TD方法的更新几何结构，在理论分析和数值实验中都展现出相对于传统方法的性能优势。该方法为off-policy预测提供了新的思路，特别是在行为策略转移信息能够改善鞍点几何结构的情况下表现更优。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [13] [Behavior-Aware Auxiliary Corrections for Off-Policy Temporal-Difference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28855)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhiang He,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BA-TDC和BA-TDRC两种行为感知的离策略TD学习算法，通过替换辅助协方差矩阵为行为贝尔曼矩阵并引入正则化，实现了更稳定的学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在函数近似下，时序差分学习在离策略采样时可能不稳定。现有TDC通过辅助协方差修正稳定离策略TD，TDRC进一步在单时间尺度递归中正则化该修正。本文研究在用于理解值函数近似特征空间动力学的标准局部模型——线性预测设定下，用行为感知方法替换辅助协方差几何。

Method: 首先将TDC的辅助矩阵C替换为行为贝尔曼矩阵A_μ，得到BA-TDC；然后对同一行为感知方程进行正则化，得到BA-TDRC。这种两步构造分离了行为感知几何的贡献和正则化的贡献。给出了有限状态均值系统表述，证明了不动点保持性和在Hurwitz稳定性条件下的几乎必然收敛性。

Result: 实验表明：在两状态反例、Baird反例、Random Walk和Boyan Chain上，行为感知替换在某些任务上本身就有显著优势，但在更困难的环境中需要正则化才能实现鲁棒性能。通过精确线性误差递归的谱半径比较了确定性均值收敛率。

Conclusion: 行为感知的辅助几何设计可以有效改善离策略TD学习的稳定性，但需要配合正则化机制来保证跨任务的鲁棒性能。该工作为神经网络值近似中的辅助几何设计问题提供了可处理的理论模型。

Abstract: Temporal-difference learning with function approximation can be unstable under off-policy sampling. TDC stabilizes off-policy TD through an auxiliary covariance correction, and TDRC further regularizes this correction in a single-timescale recursion. This paper studies a behavior-aware replacement of the auxiliary covariance geometry in the linear prediction setting, which is the standard local model for understanding the feature-space dynamics of value-function approximation. We first replace the TDC auxiliary matrix (C) by the behavior Bellman matrix (A_μ), yielding BA-TDC, and then regularize the same behavior-aware equation to obtain BA-TDRC. This two-step construction separates the contribution of behavior-aware geometry from the contribution of regularization. The linear analysis also provides a tractable model for an auxiliary-geometry design question that arises in neural-network value approximation, where feature covariances and temporal transition matrices jointly shape the last-layer correction dynamics. We give a finite-state mean-system formulation, prove fixed-point preservation and almost-sure convergence under a Hurwitz stability condition on the instantiated mean system, and compare deterministic mean rates through the spectral radius of the exact linear error recursion. Experiments on the two-state counterexample, Baird's counterexample, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain show that the behavior-aware replacement can be highly beneficial by itself on some tasks, but that regularization is necessary for robust performance across harder settings.

</details>


### [14] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了认知范畴Transformer（CCT），在GPT-2 Small基础上引入范畴论和认知科学启发的组件。在WikiText-103上达到21.27验证困惑度，比微调基线降低12%。消融实验表明84%的性能提升来自单纯形消息传递，首次验证了该技术在306M参数规模的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将范畴论和认知科学的理论成果整合到大语言模型中，通过结构化归纳偏置提升模型性能。研究旨在验证认知理论基础的结构改进能否超越单纯的领域内微调带来的增益。

Method: 在预训练的GPT-2 Small（306M参数）骨干网络上，增加认知启发的架构组件，包括GT-Full单纯形消息传递和精度加权预测处理器等。采用匹配步数协议（215,000优化步，匹配数据、优化器和调度）在WikiText-103上进行评估，并通过从头重训练的消融实验定位关键改进来源。

Result: CCT达到21.27验证困惑度，相比基线的24.19降低2.92 PPL（12%相对改进）。消融实验显示GT-Full单纯形消息传递贡献了84%的架构改进（2.45 PPL）。三个一致性风格的范畴先验（层平滑、伴随往返、曲率正则化）产生负面结果，揭示了"结构/一致性区分"模式：增加新拓扑的先验有效，而强制一致性身份的先验无效。

Conclusion: 首次提供消融验证证据，表明单纯形消息传递在306M参数规模的语言模型中能有效降低困惑度。研究发现范畴先验存在结构/一致性区分：增加拓扑结构的方法能改善语言建模，而强制一致性身份的方法则无效，为未来认知启发架构设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [15] [Ultra-Reduced-Impact-Encased-Logging (URIEL): propose a new method for selective sustainable logging and post-harvest silvicultural treatment in tropical forest using airborne robotics systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28883)
*Daniel Albiero,Gelton Fernando de Morais,Daniela Han,Flávio Roberto de Freitas Gonçalves,Artur Vitório Andrade Santos,Wesllen Lins de Araújo,Alessandra Maia Freire,Cláudio Kiyoshi Umezu,Mateus Peressin,Francesco Toscano,Admilson Írio Ribeiro,Alfeu J. Sguarezi Filho,Américo Ferraz Dias Neto,Angel Pontin Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为URIEL的新型热带森林采伐方法，该方法结合直升机采伐、机器人、人工智能和无人机后期抚育技术。研究证明该方法具有很高的经济可行性，能够几乎消除对森林的附带损害并维持生态系统服务，但其实施需要多方利益相关者的协同合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球热带森林正面临由经济和政治利益驱动的严重森林砍伐压力，科学证据表明这种砍伐导致了气候变化。现有采伐方法对森林生态系统造成严重损害，因此迫切需要开发一种既能满足经济需求又能最大限度减少环境影响的可持续采伐技术。

Method: 提出了超低影响封闭式采伐方法(URIEL)，该方法基于直升机采伐技术，结合机器人和人工智能的密集应用，以及无人机执行采伐后森林抚育处理。研究开发了适用于该方法的设备概念，确定了尺寸规格，完成了数字化概念验证，并对多种直升机-木材-距离组合进行了有效的数字模拟和经济可行性分析。

Result: 研究结果表明，URIEL方法具有很高的经济可行性，能够在维持生态系统服务的同时，几乎完全消除对森林的附带损害。数字模拟和经济可行性分析验证了该方法在不同操作条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 尽管URIEL方法在科学技术方面取得了令人满意的结果，但其实际可行性取决于利益相关者的协同整合，包括高科技产业、政治政府、认证伐木公司和原住民群体。技术成功之外，社会政治因素是实现该方法的关键。

Abstract: Tropical forests worldwide are under intense deforestation pressure driven by economic and political interests, and scientific evidence suggests this deforestation contributes to climate change. This paper proposes a novel logging method for tropical forests, Ultra-Reduced-Impact-Encased-Logging (URIEL). This new method is based on heli-logging techniques combined with intensive use of robotics and AI integrated with post-harvest silvicultural treatments performed by drones. The concept of appropriate equipment for this method was developed, dimensions were determined, details were completed in a digital proof of concept, and an effective digital simulation and economic feasibility analysis were carried out for various helicopter-timber-distance combinations. The results demonstrated that a URIEL method has high economic viability and makes it possible to virtually eliminate collateral damage to forests while maintaining ecosystem services. The main conclusion of this paper is that, despite the satisfactory scientific and technological results, the feasibility of a Uriel method depends on the integration of stakeholders intrinsic to the context: high-tech industry; political governments; certified logging companies; and native populations.

</details>


### [16] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM生成的论文评审与人类评审的一致性，发现一致性有限且随提示词和模型变化较大；同时发现作者利用LLM进行迭代修改可以“博弈”评审系统，在特定场景下显著提高论文评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成的论文评审被主要会议试点采用，评审者和作者都可能使用LLM辅助。需要从作者和评审者双重视角评估LLM评审的质量和潜在被“博弈”的风险。

Method: 基于2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文进行实证实验，首先评估LLM评审与人类评审的一致性，测试不同提示词和模型的影响；然后模拟作者使用LLM进行迭代“起草-修改”工作流，根据LLM评审建议修改论文以提升评分。

Result: LLM评审与人类评审的一致性有限，最佳情况下尚可接受，但在不同提示词和模型间差异显著。作者利用LLM迭代修改可以“博弈”评审，在特定场景下导致最多35%的论文总体评分出现统计学显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审存在一致性不稳定的问题，且存在被作者通过迭代修改“博弈”的风险，需要在实际应用中谨慎对待LLM辅助评审系统。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [17] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法，通过乘性正交变换替代加性参数更新，在4.3秒内可擦除多达100个概念，同时保持模型整体生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编辑类概念擦除方法采用加性参数更新，会同时影响神经元方向、幅度和角度几何，导致概念擦除精度与整体生成能力之间存在不可避免的干扰冲突。

Method: 从几何视角将概念擦除重构为乘性参数更新，使用闭式解推导层级正交变换，保持神经元幅度和角度几何不变；针对多概念擦除引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操作。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上超越现有方法，实现精确概念擦除的同时保持非目标概念的生成质量，可在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。

Conclusion: OCE通过正交乘性更新实现了概念语义擦除与生成能力保持的有效解耦，为扩散模型的安全可控部署提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [18] [Frontier LLM-based agents can overcome the ontology curation bottleneck for natural phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28965)
*James P. Balhoff,Hilmar Lapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五个前沿大语言模型作为“代理策展人”在表型注释任务中的表现，发现所有模型的性能均落在人类策展人的变异范围内，且显著优于之前的NLP工具，但未达到最佳人类策展人的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表型注释（将自由文本表型描述链接到本体术语）对于比较形态学数据的跨研究整合至关重要，但传统方法依赖高度训练的人类专家，劳动密集且难以扩展，成为关键瓶颈。先前研究表明机器与人类的一致性显著低于人类之间的一致性，因此需要探索新技术来提升自动化注释的性能。

Method: 使用Anthropic和OpenAI的五个前沿托管LLM作为“代理策展人”，在自包含工作空间中运行，提供源出版物PDF、原始人类策展人使用的注释指南、四个项目本体（UBERON、PATO、BSPO、GO）和验证脚本。采用Dahdul等人（2018）建立的金标准（GS）Entity-Quality（EQ）注释基准，使用基于本体的语义相似性度量进行评估。

Result: 在相同的金标准评估下，每个代理的性能都落在原始研究中三位训练有素的人类生物策展人的策展人间变异范围内；表现最好的代理接近但未达到表现最好的人类策展人水平。在所有四个指标上，代理均显著优于Semantic CharaParser。

Conclusion: 前沿大语言模型作为代理策展人在表型注释任务中表现出与人类专家相当的竞争力，显著优于传统NLP工具，为自动化表型注释提供了新的可行方案，有助于突破该领域的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Linking free-text phenotype descriptions to ontology terms, typically referred to as phenotype annotation, is essential for the cross-study integration of comparative morphological data. This labor intensive process has heavily relied on highly trained human experts, which makes it challenging to scale and thus a key bottleneck. Dahdul et al. (2018) established a Gold Standard (GS) of Entity-Quality (EQ) annotations across seven phylogenetic studies and used it to evaluate three human curators and the Semantic CharaParser NLP tool with ontology-based semantic similarity metrics; they reported that machine-human consistency was significantly lower than inter-curator (human-human) consistency. Here we revisit that benchmark with five frontier hosted LLMs from Anthropic and OpenAI, each operating as an "agentic curator" within a self-contained workspace that supplies the source publication PDF, the same annotation guide used by the original human curators, the four project ontologies (UBERON, PATO, BSPO, GO), and a validation script. Evaluated against the same Gold Standard, every agent fell within the range of inter-curator variability of the three trained human biocurators of the original study; the best performing agents approached but did not reach the best performing human curator. Agents substantially outperformed Semantic CharaParser on all four metrics.

</details>


### [19] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VFEAgent，一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够直接从输入图像和问题描述自动完成有限元分析（FEA）建模与仿真，通过多模态视觉-语言管道和验证优先的代码合成框架，实现了高成功率的完整且物理有效的仿真生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）工作流程复杂且严重依赖领域专业知识。尽管近期已有将大语言模型（LLM）集成到FEA中的尝试，但现有方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限性。

Method: 提出了VFEAgent系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 多模态视觉-语言多智能体管道，采用ReAct驱动的推理从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规范；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，集成健壮的自调试和回退机制以确保可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 系统在各种工程力学场景中进行了系统评估。结果表明，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面达到了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性方面优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究验证了自动化完整FEA工作流程的可行性，突显了该框架将工程师从繁琐手工分析中解放出来的潜力。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [20] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议通过开放基础设施和自动化测试评估AI建模与仿真工具，发现AI工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复方面表现较弱，且没有单一LLM在所有任务上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持现实世界决策的AI工具需要构建仿真模型来提供建议并使其具有可解释性，而自动化建模工具应补充而非替代人类专业知识。因此需要建立以人为本的建模与仿真实践基准，指导负责任和道德的AI工具开发。

Method: BEAMS倡议使用开放的数字和组织基础设施协作评估AI建模与仿真工具。通过开源sd ai项目建立透明度，设立指导组优先考虑潜在基准，技术组以自动化测试形式实现基准。测试涵盖多个评估类别：因果转换、模型迭代、因果推理、合规性、模型行为解释、建议建模步骤和建议模型修复，应用于支持定性建模、定量建模和模型讨论的AI工具。

Result: 评估显示AI建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现优于因果推理和定量错误修复。不同LLM与sd ai项目引擎结合时，各AI工具性能存在差异。没有单一LLM在所有引擎类型中占主导地位，凸显了特定任务的重要性以及速度与准确性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议的评估证明了AI建模工具的能力边界和改进方向。未来工作将继续纳入解决偏见问题的基准，通过考虑替代视角和以人为本的用例来完善评估体系，推动负责任的AI建模工具发展。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [21] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究分析了约12,000名Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，发现用户行为习惯具有高度粘性，个体层面的变化趋势微弱，且WildChat数据集偏向高 proficiency 用户，不能代表典型用户行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对用户与LLM交互的描述主要是静态的，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间变化的理解。研究旨在填补这一空白，探究用户行为的时间演变特征。

Method: 分析约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行比较分析，研究个体用户行为变化趋势。

Result: 发现用户习惯具有高度粘性，个体用户轨迹的趋势远弱于群体层面趋势。活跃用户对话更成功，使用场景更复杂且偏职业化。WildChat数据集显著偏向高熟练度的"重度"用户，存在代表性偏差。

Conclusion: 现有用户行为难以改变，用户异质性显著。WildChat数据集不能代表典型的用户-AI交互，这对下游数据使用具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [22] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了解释性审计管道，将多模型分歧视为解释复杂性的诊断指标，用于评估联邦机构使用大语言模型分类公众评论语料库的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构正在使用大语言模型对公众评论语料库进行分类，模型对记录的组织方式会影响决策者所看到的内容以及哪些论点会被记录。标准的基于立场准确率的评估方法无法检测不同模型对同一公众输入产生实质性不同分类的情况，因此需要新的评估方法。

Method: 提出解释性审计管道，将多模型分歧视为解释复杂性的诊断指标，并将人工审查引导至真正模糊的公众输入。分析了USDA联邦档案中1,260条公众评论，使用四个LLM进行标注，并通过两阶段标注研究，让四个LLM和一名人类标注员独立标注后在看到他人标签后进行修订。

Result: 发现模型间主题分歧超过了模型内提示变体，专家规则抑制了深层的解释分歧但并未解决分歧。在修订阶段，不同标注者的行为存在差异，人类标注员的修订经常引入了集成模型集体输出中不存在的框架。

Conclusion: 基于分歧的评估是LLM辅助解释性编码的准确率指标的必要补充，标准准确率指标不足以评估LLM在公共评论分类任务中的表现。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [23] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统调查了提示语调变化对四个主流大语言模型在客观多选题任务上准确性的影响，发现语调效应具有系统性但高度依赖模型，并提出了路由框架解释语调如何影响模型内部推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的使用日益普及，但观察发现其性能会随提示风格和语调变化而波动。目前尚不清楚语调变化是否以及如何导致LLM在客观多选题上的准确性差异，这对LLM部署的可靠性提出了挑战。

Method: 使用两个数据集进行实验：(1) 包含5个语调变体的50题基础数据集；(2) 跨越57个学科、包含7个语调变体的570题MMLU子集。评估了四个高性价比主流LLM：ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite的性能表现。

Result: 跨模型分析显示语调效应具有系统性但高度依赖具体模型：部分模型呈现小但统计显著的准确性变化，而其他模型在不同语调下出现大幅准确率波动。研究还发现了学科层面的语调敏感性差异，并提出了路由框架来解释语调如何调节内部推理模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，语调对LLM性能的影响不容忽视。用户在部署LLM时不应假设其对语调变化具有鲁棒的可靠性，这为LLM的实际应用提供了重要的警示和指导。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [24] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，结合连续记忆系统和语义相似性缓存，通过三阶段智能体流水线显著降低了LLM系统中的幻觉现象（THS降低31.3%-35.9%），同时语义缓存实现了47.3%的命中率，大幅减少了LLM调用次数和碳足迹，证明了记忆增强的多智能体设计可以在无需模型重训练的情况下同时提升事实可靠性、运营效率和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉仍然是生产级LLM系统的主要可靠性障碍，特别是在多智能体流水线中，不受支持的声明可能在各阶段间无约束传播，导致系统可靠性下降。现有研究缺乏对多阶段流水线中幻觉传播和缓解机制的系统评估。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，集成了连续记忆系统（CMS）和语义相似性缓存机制；构建了包含310个提示词的混合基准测试（217个认知不确定性提示词+93个虚构诱导压力测试提示词）；设计了基于开放楼层协议（OFP）的三阶段智能体流水线，包括高随机性前端生成器（temperature=1.0）作为幻觉基线源、以及二级和三级审查器作为渐进式纠正器；使用五个KPI指标（FCD、FGR、FDF、ECS、OSR）聚合为总幻觉评分（THS），通过五种权重配置评估缓解-可观测性权衡关系。

Result: 端到端幻觉评分在五种权重配置下实现了-31.3%到-35.9%的降低；语义缓存在930次潜在调用中实现了440次缓存命中（47.3%命中率），将LLM调用减少至490次，显著降低了能源消耗和CO2当量足迹；ExtremeObservability配置获得最负的最终THS值（-0.0709），证明可观测性密集型配置能够增强而非损害幻觉缓解效果。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的多智能体设计能够在不进行模型重训练的情况下，同时提升LLM系统的事实可靠性、运营效率和可审计性；高可观测性配置不仅不会妥协幻觉缓解效果，反而能够强化缓解能力；多阶段审查流水线结合语义缓存在生产规模下具有实际可行性，为构建可靠的LLM系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [25] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过调查72名高等教育从业者对AI教学整合的态度与实践，发现从业者普遍认可AI的教学支持作用，同时强调人工监督的重要性；实践上侧重迭代提示与内容生成，但在需求评估和反馈循环方面不足；政策、培训和基础设施等制度障碍普遍存在，初步验证了DOT框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术在教育领域的快速发展，需要了解高等教育从业者对AI教学整合的观念、行为及制度环境现状，以期为理论模型验证和实践改进提供实证依据。

Method: 基于整合设计思维和开放系统理论的DOT框架，对72名高等教育从业者进行横断面调查，探究AI熟悉度、使用模式、设计导向实践和教学信念，并对19个信念题项进行探索性因子分析。

Result: 探索性因子分析识别出三因子结构：AI功能能力、监督与治理、教师协作与规划（α=.90）。从业者对AI持积极态度，但强调人工监督与批判性评估；实践侧重迭代提示与内容生成，需求评估和反馈循环使用不足；制度障碍包括政策、培训和基础设施的缺乏。

Conclusion: 研究为DOT框架作为描述从业者信念与实践的模型提供了初步实证支持，揭示了设计导向理论与当前实践之间的差距，贡献了初步测量结构，并为后续验证性验证和基于结果的AI支持设计实践与教学质量关联研究指明方向。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [26] [Why Specialist Models Still Matter: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Paradigm for Medical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29744)
*Yanan Wang,Shuaicong Hu,Jian Liu,Guohui Zhou,Aiguo Wang,Cuiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HetMedAgent多智能体框架，通过协调通用LLM、领域专家模型和临床医生，证明专家模型不可替代且协同效果显著优于单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型（如GPT、Claude）在医疗领域的优异表现引发质疑：领域特定的医疗专家模型是否会过时？作者认为未来不应构建单一医疗基础模型，而应实现多方协作。

Method: 提出异构医疗多智能体框架HetMedAgent，具备冲突感知证据融合、基于不确定性的临床医生干预触发机制以及自适应阈值校准能力，实现通用LLM与领域专家模型的协同工作。

Result: 在三个真实临床决策任务上的实验表明，通用LLM与领域专家模型的协同显著优于单独使用任一类型模型，验证了专家模型在模态特定分析中的不可替代价值。

Conclusion: 医疗AI的未来在于多智能体协作而非构建单一医疗LLM，HetMedAgent实现了通用推理能力与领域特定精度的平衡，代表了从模型构建到智能体协作的范式转变。

Abstract: The impressive performance of generalist large language models (LLMs) such as GPT and Claude in healthcare raises a critical question: will domain-specific medical specialist models become obsolete? We argue that the future of medical artificial intelligence (AI) lies not in building monolithic medical foundation models, nor in replacing human expertise, but in orchestrating collaboration among generalist LLMs, domain-specific specialist models, and clinicians. We propose HetMedAgent, a heterogeneous medical multi-agent framework that enables conflict-aware evidence fusion, uncertainty-based clinician intervention triggering, and adaptive threshold calibration. Experiments on three real-world clinical decision-making tasks demonstrate that the synergy between generalist LLMs and domain-specific specialist models significantly outperforms using either type of model alone, validating the irreplaceable value of specialist models in modality-specific analysis. HetMedAgent represents a shift from building medical LLMs or foundation models to multi-agent collaboration, achieving a balance between general reasoning capabilities and domain-specific precision.

</details>


### [27] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了D-BOS方法，通过在信念空间中进行可微分的对手塑造，解决了多智能体强化学习中对手塑造和隐藏角色游戏中信念操控的问题，在混合动机设置中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手塑造方法通常在对手的参数、策略或价值空间中操作，而隐藏角色游戏中的信念操控技术往往依赖硬编码目标（如欺骗或信念饱和）。缺乏一种能够自然地利用环境奖励结构来塑造对手信念的方法。

Method: 提出可微分基于信念的对手塑造方法（D-BOS），这是一种一阶方法，将每个观察者的信念视为被塑造的对手状态，并通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动态进行微分。该方法不显式奖励欺骗或合作行为，而是将信念状态作为塑造目标，通过对手信念更新的微分来提供对手塑造信号。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS在性能上优于PPO和BBM，在混合动机设置中获得最大收益。该方法能够自然扩展到多个观察者，通过聚合各个推断信念轨迹的梯度来处理多观察者场景。

Conclusion: 基于信念空间的可微分对手塑造方法为多智能体强化学习提供了新的研究方向，能够从环境奖励结构中自然涌现最优策略，避免了硬编码目标的局限性。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [28] [On the Geometry of Games and their Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29919)
*Yaqi Sun,Julian Ma,David Mguni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构感知的求解器合成框架，通过学习游戏的结构表示来自适应地选择求解机制，从而解决了传统离散分类方法无法有效刻画不同求解器适用范围的局限性，揭示了博弈空间中求解器有效性的连续分布规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博弈论和生成对抗网络（GANs）等学习系统面临的核心挑战是理解哪些算法能够在异构博弈环境中高效计算均衡解。现有研究采用逐个求解器和逐类博弈的方式进行分析，虽然提供了强局部保证，但导致求解器行为的理解呈现碎片化。传统离散分类方法无法完整刻画算法成功的条件，经典理论仅识别出孤立区域，对中间或重叠区域缺乏深入洞察。

Method: 提出了结构感知求解器合成框架，包含两个核心组件：1）学习型结构识别器将每个博弈映射到低维求解器对齐表示；2）策略网络将此表示映射到有效的基本机制，实现跨区域的求解器行为自适应。引入有界残差作为局部校正器和诊断信号，用于检测不完备的求解器基或表示。该框架同时提供自适应求解器和分析视角。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）固定基本机制存在系统性区域失配问题；2）学习到的表示将博弈空间组织成与求解器行为对齐的结构化图谱；3）框架能够揭示特定求解器动态有效区域以及需要多种基本机制混合而非单一主导求解器的区域；4）具有相似优化动态的博弈聚集在一起，展现了算法有效性的连续区域和求解器行为的重叠特征。

Conclusion: 均衡计算应被重新审视为学习求解器机制与映射可解性几何的联合问题。研究揭示了博弈空间中存在由潜在结构性质支配的连续求解器对齐几何，为理解异构博弈环境中算法行为的连续性变化提供了新的理论视角和分析工具，对指导均衡求解算法设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: A central challenge in game theory and learning systems such as GANs is understanding which algorithms can efficiently compute equilibria across the heterogeneous landscape of games. Equilibrium computation is typically studied solver by solver and game class by game class, yielding strong local guarantees but a fragmented view of solver behaviour. Existing discrete taxonomies often provide an incomplete account of where algorithms succeed. We study this problem through a solver-game map linking games to effective solver dynamics. Classical theory identifies isolated regions of this map but provides limited insight into intermediate or overlapping regimes, suggesting that solvability is governed by latent structural properties defining a continuous solver-aligned geometry of games. We formalise this perspective through structure-aware solver synthesis. A learned structure recogniser maps each game to a low-dimensional solver-aligned representation, and a policy maps this representation to effective primitive mechanisms, adapting solver behaviour across regimes. This reveals regions where particular solver dynamics are effective and where mixtures of primitives are required rather than a single dominant solver. A bounded residual acts as a local corrector and diagnostic signal for incomplete solver bases or representations. The framework yields both an adaptive solver and an analytical lens: games with similar optimisation dynamics cluster together, revealing continuous regions of algorithmic validity and overlapping solver behaviour. Empirically, we show that fixed primitives exhibit systematic regime mismatch, while the learned representation organises game space into a structured cartography aligned with solver behaviour. These results suggest viewing equilibrium computation as the joint problem of learning solver mechanisms and mapping the geometry of solvability.

</details>


### [29] [AgentSchool: An LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Simulation for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30144)
*Yulei Ye,Wenhao Li,Zhong Wen,Yunshu Huang,Yichen Hu,Zifan Wei,Yige Wang,Xinyu Xie,Haoxuan Yang,Yanjun Huang,Ruijia Li,Hong Qian,Yu Song,Bo Jiang,Bingdong Li,Lijun Li,Bo Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Xingcheng Xu,Shuangye Chen,Xia Hu,Liang He,Aimin Zhou,Jingjing Qu,Jing Shao,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AgentSchool，一个基于LLM的多智能体教育模拟器，通过将学习建模为状态转移（而非简单的角色扮演），解决了教育AI验证困难的问题，并能生成符合教育理论的认知和社会动态轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育AI的验证面临独特挑战：干预会影响处于发展中的学习者，造成不可逆的认知和社会轨迹改变，而真实世界的试验缓慢、受伦理约束且受机构限制。现有的基于LLM的教育模拟器多将学习简化为基于人设的角色扮演，且仅优化以复现现有课堂，会结构性惩罚教学改革所需的制度创新。

Method: AgentSchool是一个LLM驱动的多智能体模拟器，将学习建模为状态转移而非提示行为。系统包含：(1)认知可成长的学生智能体，配备加权学科知识图谱、思维工作流池和显性错误概念；(2)适应性教师智能体，沿最近发展区(ZPD)进行规划、支架搭建和反思；(3)可配置场景生成器，将教学置于正式和非正式学习场域中；(4)多尺度模拟器，解耦交互规模、时间粒度和模拟持续时间。

Result: 实验表明：(1)结构化的学生智能体比基线模拟器产生更分化的掌握度和错误概念轨迹；(2)教师智能体比较显示出与ZPD指导的适应相一致的主干依赖模式；(3)AgentSchool生成了符合课堂社会理论的合理轨迹，包括边缘参与、派系形成、攻击者诱导的凝聚和意见领袖涌现等社会现象。

Conclusion: AgentSchool不仅作为教育研究工具，还将教育定位为长时域记忆、多智能体协调和组织压力下未来制度推理的具有社会意义的测试平台。这为教育AI的验证提供了一条可行路径，同时为多智能体系统研究提供了新的实验环境。

Abstract: Despite the rapid deployment of LLMs into classrooms, validating educational AI remains uniquely intractable: interventions act on developing learners whose cognitive and social trajectories are irreversibly shaped, while real-world trials are slow, ethically constrained, and institutionally locked. LLM-based educational simulators have emerged as a potential remedy, but many still collapse learning into persona-conditioned role-play and, when optimized only to reproduce existing classrooms, can structurally penalize the institutional novelty that pedagogical reform requires. In this work, we introduce AgentSchool, an LLM-driven multi-agent simulator that models learning as state transition rather than prompted behavior. AgentSchool couples cognitively growable student agents -- equipped with weighted subject knowledge graphs, thinking-workflow pools, and explicit misconceptions -- with adaptive teacher agents that plan, scaffold, and reflect along the Zone of Proximal Development, embedded in a configurable scenery generator that situates instruction within both formal and informal learning fields, and a multi-scale simulator that decouples interaction scale, temporal granularity, and simulation duration. Experiments show that structured student agents produce more differentiated mastery and misconception traces than a baseline simulator, while teacher-agent comparisons show backbone-dependent patterns consistent with ZPD-informed adaptation. Further, AgentSchool generates plausible traces of peripheral participation, clique formation, aggressor-induced cohesion, and opinion-leader emergence consistent with classroom social theories. Beyond its role as an educational research instrument, AgentSchool frames education as a socially meaningful testbed for long-horizon memory, multi-agent coordination, and future institutional reasoning under organizational pressure.

</details>


### [30] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COLAGUARD，一种将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间的护栏模型，在匹配显式推理基线性能的同时实现了12.9倍加速和22.4倍的token使用减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于推理的安全护栏虽然性能优越，但存在显著的查询延迟和token开销问题，使其在高吞吐量部署场景中不切实际。现有方法在安全鲁棒性和推理效率之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，通过分阶段训练课程将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间中，实现在推理时直接进行隐藏状态传播，从而避免显式的逐步推理过程。

Result: 在涵盖八个安全基准的十个提示和响应审核设置上评估，COLAGUARD相比Llama Guard 3宏F1提升8.24个百分点；与显式推理基线GuardReasoner性能匹配的同时实现12.9倍加速和22.4倍的token使用减少。

Conclusion: 潜在推理为可部署护栏提供了实用的显式理由生成替代方案，能够同时提升安全鲁棒性和推理效率，而非将二者视为竞争目标。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [31] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出策略无关的执行与测量层，解决工业环境中事件驱动调度策略面临的异步与部分可观测问题，将模糊的执行错误转化为可归因的结构化结果，提升系统可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中事件驱动调度策略在异步和部分可观测的系统状态下进行决策，导致决策状态时间不一致、动作可容许性未明确定义、执行错误来源模糊，限制了系统的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 构建策略无关的执行与测量层，介于调度策略与工业执行环境之间，从异步事件流构建决策有效快照，定义具有明确动作可容许性的标准化执行契约，并将策略意图、事务结果、物理执行和人工干预之间的偏差作为结果记录，实现决策语义与执行行为的分离。

Result: 通过离散事件仿真评估，所有观测延迟机制下均获得分析收益，未区分的执行失败被转化为具有完整归因覆盖的结构化类型结果；在低观测延迟下运营收益最强，可避免的执行错误可在提交前被阻止。

Conclusion: 该执行与测量层将执行不确定性转化为监督数据，用于策略评估与优化，使部署不匹配变得可观测且可结构化归因，显著提升了工业调度系统的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>

*Tyler Akidau,Tyler Rockwood,Johannes Brüderl,Marc Millstone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP)架构，通过带外元数据通道在AI代理生命周期中强制执行治理，解决AI代理在不可预测性与高技术能力结合时带来的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理正日益作为数字员工在环境中操作企业数据并自主决策，但其不可预测性（易产生幻觉、误解、遭受对抗性操控）与高技术能力（深度系统知识、高通量接口）的结合，导致依赖代理忠实解释或传播安全关键元数据（如访问策略、数据分类、行为约束）变得不安全，存在机器级联损害的风险。

Method: 提出Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP)架构，核心是构建带外元数据通道：完全独立于代理读写路径的基础设施路径，跨异构基础设施确定性地传输安全上下文、策略信号和审计轨迹。在代理生命周期的每个阶段强制执行治理：输入时限定数据访问范围、执行时约束行为、输出时捕获防篡改记录。

Result: 通过多代理投资组合再平衡系统进行验证，自主代理在隔离的客户账户间监控市场、做出交易决策并执行订单。带外通道强制实现了按客户的数据范围限定、交易审批阈值以及防篡改审计轨迹，且代理无法查看或绕过这些通道。

Conclusion: 带外元数据通道架构能够在不依赖代理自身可靠性的前提下，为AI代理系统提供确定性的安全治理与审计能力，有效解决AI代理在安全关键场景中的信任与风险控制问题。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly expected to operate as digital employees: accessing enterprise data, making decisions, and taking actions autonomously. But agents are simultaneously less predictable than humans -- prone to hallucination, misinterpretation, and adversarial manipulation -- and more technically capable: with deep system knowledge and high-throughput interfaces cascading damage at machine speed. This combination makes it unsafe to rely on agents to faithfully interpret or propagate security-critical metadata such as access policies, data classifications, and behavioral constraints.
  We present the Redpanda Agentic Data Plane (ADP), an architecture built around out-of-band metadata channels: infrastructure pathways that carry security context, policy signals, and audit trails deterministically, entirely outside the agent's read and write path and across heterogeneous infrastructure. These channels enforce governance at every stage of the agent lifecycle -- scoping data access on the way in, constraining actions during execution, and capturing tamper-proof transcripts on the way out.
  We demonstrate ADP with a multi-agent portfolio rebalancing system in which autonomous agents monitor markets, make trade decisions, and execute orders across isolated client accounts -- with per-client data scoping, trade approval thresholds, and tamper-proof audit trails all enforced by out-of-band channels the agents can neither see nor bypass.

</details>

*Yubo Li,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现推理模型在多轮对抗对话中存在“不真实屈服”（UC）现象：思维链保持正确但最终答案翻转为错误；提出了潜在-行为框架来识别这一单轮评估无法捕捉的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型评估主要基于单轮基准测试，但实际部署于多轮对话场景中，用户会对正确答案提出质疑。研究旨在探究模型在持续对抗压力下的行为表现，填补评估与部署场景之间的差距。

Method: 提出2×2潜在状态vs行为表现框架来隔离UC现象；在三个数据集（MT-Consistency、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K）上测试；对比think和no_think两种模式；使用GPT-4o作为独立评判者验证UC标签；采用token级探测分析答案槽位的argmax分布。

Result: think模式下潜在正确但行为翻转率接近50%，no_think模式下降至11-15%，证明推理过程造成差距；不同模型表现差异显著（Qwen3-32B和GPT-OSS-20B高，Gemma-4-31B-it低）；GPT-4o评判者验证了86%的UC标签；84%的UC样本中答案槽位argmax实际正确；简单的trace锚定防御产生反效果。

Conclusion: UC是一种此前未被记录的失效模式，传统的翻转率指标和单轮忠实性探测都无法识别；推理通道的引入反而可能加剧答案与思维链之间的不一致性，需要开发更有效的防御策略。

Abstract: Reasoning models are evaluated on single-turn benchmarks but deployed in multi-turn dialogue, where users push back on correct answers. Under sustained adversarial pressure we find a previously undocumented failure mode: the chain-of-thought stays factually correct from first turn to last while the emitted answer flips wrong. We call this unfaithful capitulation (UC) and isolate it with a $2\times 2$ latent-versus-behavioral framework that flip-rate metrics and single-turn faithfulness probes both miss. Across three datasets (MT-Consistency, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K), the latent-correct rate at the behavioral flip clusters near 50% in think mode and collapses to 11-15% under no_think -- paired, within-model causal evidence that reasoning creates the gap. Across models the effect tracks the reasoning channel (high in Qwen3-32B and GPT-OSS-20B, low in inline-CoT Gemma-4-31B-it). An independent GPT-4o judge corroborates $86\%$ of UC labels; a token-level probe shows the answer-slot argmax is correct in $84\%$ of UC cells; and a naive trace-anchored defense backfires. We release all trajectories, traces, and judge labels.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了ClinicalTrials.gov中AI相关试验的术语演变与地理分布趋势，探索了人机协作筛选方法，发现AI试验数量显著增长且地理分布扩展，但人机交互分类一致性有待提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在医疗领域的快速发展，需要系统性地了解AI在临床试验中的应用趋势和地理分布特征，同时探索人机协作方法在临床研究数据分析中的可行性和局限性。

Method: 从ClinicalTrials.gov注册库检索AI相关试验记录，分析术语演变和地理分布的时空趋势。采用探索性人机混合工作流程，结合前沿生成式AI模型(GPT-5.5)与人工审查，对AI聚焦搜索返回的记录进行筛选和分类。

Result: AI相关试验数量随时间显著增长，近期机器学习、深度学习、聊天机器人、GPT和大语言模型等术语引用增多。地理分布上，中国和美国数量最多，意大利、法国、西班牙、英国和土耳其等国近期显著增长。在100个随机样本中，人机分类器在识别非实质性AI应用研究中一致性良好，但在人机交互分类上一致性较低，特别是在医疗专业人员交互模糊或描述不充分时。

Conclusion: 人机混合筛选临床试验记录具有潜在可行性，但需要更清晰的试验报告标准和更精确的交互定义来改进该过程，提高分类准确性和一致性。

Abstract: This paper examines records retrieved from the ClinicalTrials.gov registry to characterize temporal trends in AI terminology and the geographical distribution of AI trials. The work also reports on an exploratory hybrid human-AI approach to analyzing human-AI interaction trends in registered clinical trials. The hybrid workflow comprised a frontier generative AI model (GPT-5.5) and human review to screen and categorize records returned by an AI-focused search. The findings indicate a marked increase in AI-related trials over time, with recent growth in references to machine learning, deep learning, chatbots, GPTs, and large language models. Geographically, China and the United States accounted for the largest numbers of AI-related trials, with notable recent increases in several other countries including Italy, France, Spain, the UK and Turkey (Türkiye). In a random sample of 100 records, human and AI classifiers showed good agreement in identifying studies not substantively using AI, but lower agreement in classifying human-AI interaction, particularly where health professional interaction was ambiguous or insufficiently described. Overall, the results suggest that hybrid human-AI screening of clinical trial records is potentially viable, but clearer trial reporting and more precise interaction definitions will benefit the process.

</details>
