Conclusion: 任何AI品牌感知测量都必须以买家角色为条件：相同提示词会因模型判断的提问者身份不同而产生实质性不同的推荐集合。现有聚合分析协议会系统性地掩盖这种变异性。效应集中在中端市场品牌，且在更依赖训练先验的生成路径上最为显著，这与模型对丰富上下文整合能力的增强相一致。

Abstract: The same prompt -- "best CRM software" -- reaches AI assistants from buyers in widely different contexts: a solo founder, an enterprise VP, a UK SMB owner. We audit how strongly that contextual variation reshapes which brands the model recommends. The audit samples 2,000 runs over a design space of 10 personas x 8 prompts x 3 model configurations x N=10 reps, with the two OpenAI cells at full 8-prompt coverage and the Anthropic sonnet-4.6 / low cell at 4-prompt coverage. Prefixing the user message with a persona drops the recommendation-set similarity (Jaccard) by Delta = -0.12 to -0.20 relative to a same-persona baseline (clustered 95% CIs exclude zero on all three measured cells; the sonnet cell's CI rests on only 4 prompt clusters and is correspondingly wider). The effect is sharply prominence-stratified: category leaders are persona-resistant (~80% same-brand consistency across personas), but mid-market brands swap up to 75% of the recommendation set as the persona changes. The Anthropic model shows a larger point-estimate effect than the OpenAI configurations, though clustered CIs overlap for the closer contrast (sonnet vs. OpenAI/high); the asymmetry is consistent with Anthropic's more retrieval-unattributed generation route (43-52% recommendations without observed retrieval-layer evidence, vs OpenAI's 8-29%, documented in Jack 2026). Any measurement of AI brand perception must condition on the buyer persona supplying the query: the same prompt produces materially different recommendation sets depending on who the model thinks is asking, and a measurement protocol that aggregates across personas systematically obscures that variation. The effect concentrates at mid-market and is largest on the most priors-reliant generation route in our audit, consistent with persona responsiveness growing as models lean more on training-data priors and richer context integration.

</details>


### [144] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语境信念管理（CBM）任务与BeliefTrack基准测试，发现大模型在长交互中存在严重的状态管理失败，强化学习方法可将失败率降低70.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在长时域交互中需要管理累积信息，包括何时更新状态、保持状态以及忽略无关信息，但缺乏对这一能力的系统性评估方法，因此需要研究如何量化和改进模型的信念状态管理能力。

Method: 1. 提出CBM任务框架并构建BeliefTrack基准（包含规则发现和电路诊断两个任务，具备有限信念空间和符号验证器）；
2. 定义三种失败类型：保持失败、更新失败和隔离失败；
3. 测试原始模型、显式信念追踪提示、基于信念状态的强化学习以及表示层引导四种方法。

Result: 1. 原始大模型存在严重的CBM失败问题，显式信念追踪提示改善有限；
2. 强化学习方法平均降低失败率70.9%；
3. 探测实验揭示失败背后存在潜在的信念状态动态，表示层引导可在两个任务上降低46.1%的失败率。

Conclusion: CBM是语言模型在长交互中的关键挑战，BeliefTrack提供了精确的评估方法，基于信念状态的强化学习和表示层引导是有效的改进方向，为未来研究提供了基准和思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [145] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: mcp-proto-okn是一个基于Python的MCP服务器，通过自然语言接口帮助AI助手发现、查询和集成科学知识图谱，降低了跨领域知识图谱分析的技术门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低生物医学和科学用户进行跨领域知识图谱分析的技术门槛，使AI助手能够通过自然语言而非专业查询语言来访问和利用科学知识图谱。

Method: 使用Python语言和FastMCP框架实现Model Context Protocol服务器，提供图路由、模式检查、SPARQL执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成等功能，以自然语言作为交互接口。

Result: 开发了mcp-proto-okn工具并在GitHub开源发布（https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn），提供了完整的文档、客户端配置说明和示例分析转录。

Conclusion: 该工具成功实现了AI助手与科学知识图谱的自然语言交互，为生物医学和科学计算领域提供了一个易用的跨领域知识图谱分析平台。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [146] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个渐进式科学发现评估框架，通过逐步揭示信息来测试LLM的创新推理能力，评估显示GPT-5.4在最小上下文下仍能保持0.7的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要测试LLM的多跳检索能力，而真正的科学发现所需的创新推理能力仍未经充分测试。科学发现本质上是创造性和不确定的过程，需要超越已知知识的推理，因此需要专门评估模型的科学推理和发现能力。

Method: 构建了一个渐进式评估框架，从原始问题开始逐步深入到经典零假设检验。模型初始仅接收论文主题和研究问题，技术细节逐步揭示。在每个信息揭示阶段，模型生成假设来回答研究问题，与原论文结论进行比较，通过原子声明的自动语义相似度进行评估。这种渐进式评估能够测量模型在最小信息下的创新性和完整实验细节下的推理能力。

Result: 在涵盖生物活性材料、机械材料和纳米材料的45篇论文上评估了GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro preview。结果显示GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro的表现优于前代模型，GPT-5.4在最小上下文条件下仍能保持0.7的F1分数与真实结论对齐。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理和发现能力奠定了基础，对于开发下一代AI科学家/联合科学家系统至关重要。研究证明了新世代模型在科学发现任务上的进步，特别是GPT-5.4在有限信息条件下展现出的强大推理能力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [147] [MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30288)
*Haowen Wang,Yaxin Du,Jian Yang,Jiajun Wu,Shukai Liu,Yuxuan Zhang,Pingjie Wang,Siheng Chen,Tuney Zheng,Ming Zhou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIRA框架，通过源感知的自锚定规则发现方法解决LLM中期训练的数据选择问题，在代码任务上用一半数据量达到全量数据训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期训练的数据选择面临独特挑战：需要在近预训练规模下使用预训练目标优化数据，但数据来源于不同格式和训练角色的异构源，需要兼顾可扩展性和源自适应的语义标准。现有基于模型的方法可扩展性好但质量信号隐式；语义选择方法判断力强但依赖固定规则或标准化格式，两者存在不匹配。

Method: 提出MIRA（源感知过滤框架），采用自锚定规则发现方法。核心思想是将规则构建纳入数据选择流程：首先为每个源组发现需要评估的内容，然后将这些判断蒸馏为可扩展的学生评分器，用于全语料库过滤。

Result: 在包含21个数据源和5个源组的代码导向中期训练实验中，MIRA在9个代码基准测试上超越选择基线方法，仅使用一半token就匹配了全量语料库训练的效果。

Conclusion: MIRA成功弥合了可扩展性与源自适应语义标准之间的差距，为中期训练提供了一种有效的数据选择解决方案，显著提升数据效率。

Abstract: Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.

</details>


### [148] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种数据组织优化准则（边界锐化、周期调度、课程连续性、局部多样性），并据此设计了STR和SAW两种数据排序方法，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下显著提升了大语言模型的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据选择已被广泛研究，但针对大语言模型训练的数据组织策略仍是一个未被充分探索的领域，特别是在当前LLM通常仅训练一个或几个epoch的情况下，如何有效组织数据以提升训练效率成为关键问题。

Method: 本文通过重用预计算的样本级分数（原本用于数据效率）来系统探索数据组织对LLM训练的影响，识别并形式化了四个关键优化准则：边界锐化、周期调度、课程连续性和局部多样性。基于这些准则，提出了两种新颖的数据排序方法：STR和SAW。

Result: 在不同模型规模和数据规模上进行了广泛实验，涵盖预训练和SFT阶段，验证了所总结准则的有效性，并证明了所提出的数据排序方法在增强LLM训练稳定性和性能方面的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数据组织策略对LLM训练效果具有重要影响，通过遵循边界锐化、周期调度、课程连续性和局部多样性四个准则，可以有效提升模型训练的稳定性和最终性能，且几乎不增加额外计算开销。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/

</details>


### [149] [Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30335)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多组件LLM代理在组合概率声明时会出现局部一致但全局不一致的问题，本文提出了量化、预测和修复该问题的完整框架，实验表明33-94%的代理组合存在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组件LLM代理系统中，每个组件只能看到联合问题的部分信息，虽然每个组件局部概率判断是一致的，但组合起来可能违反基本概率公理，导致全局不一致性。这种局部一致但全局不一致的失败模式尚未得到正式研究。

Method: 提出组合残差eps*（到联合一致性多面体的L2距离）来量化全局不一致性；建立乘积结构二分法定理刻画局部一致性何时充分；使用Rayleigh商预测残差；采用分层Boyle-Dykstra投影进行确定性修复；设计anytime-valid e-process进行序列一致性监控。

Result: 在4个LLM、1,876个集成团上的实验显示：33-94%的团存在eps*>0，在1,770个已解决赌注下产生每注+0.115 nats的遗憾。三种直观的LLM端缓解策略（检索、分区感知提示、聚合器LLM）均失败或退步。Rayleigh商预测在四类关系中的三类上误差在7%以内。

Conclusion: 局部一致的LLM组件组合后可能产生全局不一致性，这是多组件LLM代理系统的根本性挑战。传统直觉性的缓解方法无效，需要采用形式化的数学方法（如投影修复）来保证概率一致性。

Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [150] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou,Muntasir Wahed,Tianjiao Yu,Constantin Brif,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了包含高质量异常解释的VisAnomBench基准，并基于此微调出VisAnomReasoner模型，在时序异常检测任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在时序数据异常检测任务上表现不佳，且公开基准缺乏自然语言解释标注，难以进行有效的模型微调和可解释性决策。

Method: 基于公开时序数据集构建VisAnomBench基准，通过细粒度任务特定奖励从多个大型VLM中选择高质量异常解释，并采用参数高效方法微调VLM。

Result: VisAnomReasoner在VisAnomBench上precision和F1分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点；在TSB-AD-U跨基准测试中分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点。

Conclusion: 高质量解释标注基准结合参数高效微调能有效提升VLM在时序异常检测任务上的定位准确性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [151] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


### [152] [Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30353)
*Nhat-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究分析了AI编程代理在开发物理模拟软件过程中的角色定位与监督需求，发现AI代理容易陷入表面修复而无法识别架构性缺陷，需要人类专家的领域知识和精心设计的监督实践来确保输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在科研编程任务中的应用日益增多，需要明确AI代理的角色定位：是工具、合作者还是独立研究者？现有研究缺乏对AI代理在实际科研项目中能力边界的定量分析，特别是在需要领域专业知识的物理模拟软件开发场景中。本文旨在通过一个真实的物理软件开发案例，系统性地评估AI代理的自主性、局限性和监督需求。

Method: 采用单案例定量研究方法（N=1），物理学家监督AI编码代理（Claude Code，使用Sonnet和Opus模型）在12个工作日、57个会话中开发CLAX-PT（一个JAX实现的可微分单圈扰动理论模块）。研究者记录并分类了15个监督事件，按干预级别进行划分，分析代理自主解决、需要领域知识协助、以及无法解决的问题特征。通过oracle测试、多参数点测试、共享变更日志和明确的物理规则来实施监督。

Result: AI代理自主解决了10个问题（通过迭代oracle测试），2个问题通过物理学家的领域知识解决，3个问题无法解决。关键发现：（1）无法解决的3个问题都具有相同特性——代理将症状缓解误认为根本原因解决，在无法表示目标物理的代码架构中花费33个会话调整系数；（2）代理无法重新评估其架构选择，即使被提示重新考虑；（3）代理曾引入一个通过所有oracle测试但无物理意义的校准修正，在其他宇宙学参数下预测错误值。三种监督实践对捕获oracle测试遗漏的问题至关重要：在标定参数点之外的多样化参数点测试、跨会话共享变更日志以识别停滞的探索、以及明确禁止非物理数值补丁的规则。

Conclusion: 在本案例中，监督设计而非模型能力决定了AI代理输出是否可信。当前AI代理缺乏提出架构替代方案（而非在给定结构内优化）的能力，也缺乏区分预测充分性与解释正确性的能力——这些能力在研究中未被观察到，且仅靠模型规模扩展可能无法明显改善。AI代理当前更适合作为需要监督的工具，而非独立研究者或平等的合作者。要缩小能力差距，需要开发能够进行架构反思和物理意义验证的AI系统。

Abstract: Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level.
  The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology.
  The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Constant Depth Threshold Circuits For Exhaustive Epistasis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29719)
*André Ribeiro,Aleksandar Ilic,Leonel Sousa*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于阈值门电路的神经形态硬件解决方案，用于高效解决生物信息学中的上位效应检测问题，实现了无额外复杂度开销的组合计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经形态硬件的发展使得基于阈值门的电路实现成为可能，而传统计算方法在复杂度上的局限性促使作者研究上位效应检测这一生物信息学中最复杂的组合问题之一。

Method: 设计了专门的流水线电路来计算所有数据集组合的相对频率，利用共存内存和可配置并行性。提出了使用LIF（Leaky Integrate and Fire）神经元的数据编码和组合生成策略，馈送恒定深度的阈值门人口计数电路，并通过组合无界扇入恒定深度阈值门电路来执行人口计数和二进制数组求和。

Result: 运行时间仅受限于需要计算的组合数量，无任何额外复杂度开销，使用对数线性空间。在对数扇入和可变精度等典型硬件约束下，人口计数电路实现对数深度和对数立方线性连接复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过神经形态硬件和阈值门电路的创新设计，成功实现了上位效应检测问题的高效解决，相比传统方法获得了复杂度优势，证明了神经形态计算在复杂组合问题上的应用潜力。

Abstract: The development of large-scale neuromorphic hardware has made practical implementations of threshold gate-based circuits a near-term possibility. The complexity advantages regarding traditional computing classes, as evidenced in the literature, have prompted us to tackle Epistasis Detection, one of the most computationally complex combinatorial problems in bioinformatics. We propose specially designed circuits that calculate the relative frequencies of all dataset combinations in an efficient pipelined fashion, taking advantage of co-located memory and configurable parallelism, obtaining complexity gains. Overall, we obtain the runtime to be bounded by the number of combinations to calculate, without any additional complexity overhead, contrary to classical approaches, using log-linear space. To accomplish this, we propose a data encoding and combination generation strategy using Leaky Integrate and Fire (LIF) neurons, that feeds a constant depth threshold gate population count circuit. Accounting for typical hardware characteristics, such as limited fan-in and variable precisions, we obtain logarithmic depth and log-cubic linear connections, for the population count circuit by composing developed unbounded fan-in constant depth threshold gate circuits to perform population count and binary array sum.

</details>


### [154] [Design-Oriented Modeling of TSV Substrate Noise Coupling to Ring VCOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29867)
*Ilias Exouzidis,Alberto Garcia-Ortiz,George Floros,Georgios Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑的设计导向方法，用于评估硅通孔（TSV）在混合信号电路中引起的衬底噪声。通过推导三端口RLGC宏模型并利用22nm FD-SOI工艺中的环形压控振荡器进行验证，量化了TSV对射频电路频谱纯度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅通孔（TSV）在3D-IC和芯粒系统中实现了密集的垂直互连，但其金属-氧化物-硅结构引入了显著的寄生耦合路径，可能降低敏感射频模块的频谱纯度。为了解决这一设计挑战，需要一种能够有效评估TSV引起衬底噪声的方法。

Method: 推导了一个信号-地TSV对的闭式解析三端口RLGC宏模型，该模型显式暴露了衬底节点。使用22 nm FD-SOI技术设计的三级环形压控振荡器（Ring VCO）进行验证，利用工艺设计套件（PDK）中的特定射频器件提供对晶体管衬底端子的直接访问以进行受控噪声注入。通过Spectre RF中的多音谐波平衡仿真量化TSV干扰源对振荡器输出频谱的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，1 GHz、0.5 Vpp的干扰源会引起-35.2 dBc的主边带杂散。灵敏度特征显示边带杂散幅度随干扰源幅度单调增加。频率扫描展示了低通耦合响应，感应杂散幅度从500 MHz处的-20.2 dBc降低到2 GHz处的-33.1 dBc。

Conclusion: 本文提出的紧凑三端口RLGC宏模型能够有效评估TSV引起的衬底噪声耦合。该方法成功量化了TSV干扰源对射频电路频谱纯度的影响规律，为混合信号集成电路设计中的TSV噪声管理提供了重要的设计指导。

Abstract: Through-silicon vias (TSVs) enable dense vertical interconnects in 3D-IC and chiplet systems, but their metal-oxide-silicon structure introduces significant parasitic coupling paths that can degrade the spectral purity of sensitive RF blocks. This paper presents a compact, design-oriented methodology for assessing TSV-induced substrate noise in mixed-signal circuits. We derive a closed-form analytical three-port RLGC macromodel for a Signal-Ground TSV pair that explicitly exposes the substrate node. The methodology is validated using a three-stage Ring VCO designed in a 22 nm FD-SOI technology, where specific RF devices from the process design kit (PDK) provide direct access to the transistor substrate terminals for controlled noise injection. Multi-tone Harmonic Balance simulations in Spectre RF quantify the impact of TSV aggressors on the oscillator's output spectrum. The results indicate that an aggressor of 1 GHz, 0.5 V$_{pp}$ induces a primary sideband spur of -35.2 dBc. Sensitivity characterization reveals that the magnitude of these sideband spurs increases monotonically with the aggressor amplitude. Furthermore, frequency sweeps demonstrate a low-pass coupling response, where the induced spur magnitude decreases from -20.2 dBc at 500 MHz to -33.1 dBc at 2 GHz.

</details>


### [155] [Precomputed 1D-CNNs for Atrial Fibrillation Detection on Tiny Smart Sensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29994)
*Lukas Einhaus,Natalie Maman,Julian Hoever,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分组卷积的新型卷积块设计，通过LUT预计算方法在FPGA上实现超低延迟、高资源效率的1D-CNN时间序列分析，在无DSP/BRAM条件下达到95% F1-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUT预计算技术虽能实现高资源效率和超低延迟的FPGA神经网络部署，但存在可扩展性差的问题。现有研究虽探索了深度可分离卷积来改善可扩展性，但仍有局限性，需要更通用的解决方案。

Method: 将深度可分离卷积方法推广至更通用的分组卷积形式，提出新型卷积块结构，并开发了指导该模块超参数选择的算法，用于优化LUT预计算的性能。

Result: 在MIT-BIH心电数据库的房颤预测任务上验证，生成的硬件加速器仅需2,844个LUT，无需DSP或BRAM资源，即可部署于AMD Spartan 7 S15芯片，F1-Score达95%。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了资源受限FPGA平台上的高效时间序列分析，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了硬件资源需求，为智能传感器系统提供了可行的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: 1D-CNNs play a crucial role for time-series analysis on tiny smart sensor systems, e.g. for biosignal analysis, predictive maintenance, or structural health monitoring. LUTbased precomputation has emerged as an interesting optimization technique to implement such neural networks on FPGAs. The core idea is to precompute all possible outputs of a neural network layer and store them directly in the lookup tables of the FPGAs. This enables highly resource-efficient networks with ultra-low latency but suffers from poor scalability. Previous work has explored using depthwise-separable convolutions to improve scalability. In this paper, we generalize this approach to consider additional forms of grouped convolutions. Based on this, we propose a novel type of convolutional block and an algorithm to guide the choice of hyper parameters for this block. We evaluate our approach on a medical time-series dataset for predicting atrial fibrillation using the MIT-BIH database (ECG recordings). The resulting hardware accelerators are small enough to be deployed on an AMD Spartan 7 S15. They achieve a F1-Score of up to 95% while only requiring 2,844 LUTs and no DSPs or BRAM.

</details>


### [156] [elasticAI.explorer: Towards a Unified End-to-End Framework for Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30019)
*Natalie Maman,Florian Hettstedt,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出elasticAI.explorer，一个基于Optuna的可扩展硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，通过YAML定义搜索空间，支持硬件环路的自动化部署和评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS框架将搜索空间定义、模型实现和部署流程紧密耦合，导致扩展到新硬件平台和自定义算子困难，工程开销大。

Method: 开发基于Optuna的Python框架，引入YAML搜索空间规范，在采样时动态转换为可执行模型；支持层级、单元级和层次化搜索空间；集成硬件特定代码生成、Docker跨编译工具链和设备端基准测试二进制文件自动创建。

Result: 实现了统一的优化和部署接口，支持FLOPs、参数量和延迟估计等可扩展评估器，能够进行硬件环路的NAS工作流程。

Conclusion: elasticAI.explorer减少了嵌入式AI部署的工程开销，加速了面向异构加速器平台的硬件感知NAS研究。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has become an important approach for automatically designing neural networks under task-specific and hardware-specific constraints. However, many existing NAS frameworks tightly couple search space definitions, model implementations, and deployment pipelines, making extension to new hardware platforms and custom operators difficult. In this paper, we present the elasticAI.explorer, an extensible Python framework for hardware-aware NAS built on top of Optuna. The framework introduces a YAML-based search space specification that dynamically translates into executable neural network models during sampling. The approach supports layer-wise, cell-based, and hierarchical search spaces while maintaining a unified interface for optimization and deployment. Beyond architecture generation, the framework integrates hardware-specific code generation, Docker-based cross-compilation toolchains, and automated creation of on-device benchmarking binaries, enabling hardware-in-the-loop NAS workflows. The system further provides extensible evaluators for FLOPs, parameter count, and latency estimation. The elasticAI.explorer aims to reduce the engineering overhead of embedded AI deployment and accelerate research on hardware-aware NAS for heterogeneous accelerator platforms

</details>

### [157] [HPC-vQPU: A Service-Export Architecture for Virtual QPUs on Batch-Scheduled HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28845)
*Shusen Liu,Pascal Jahan Elahi,Ugo Varetto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HPC-vQPU架构，在批量调度的HPC系统上实现虚拟QPU服务，解决了安全超级计算机环境下量子仿真服务的交互性与设备语义保真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备感知的量子仿真日益需要HPC级加速器，但安全的超级计算机仅暴露批量调度执行环境，无法提供量子软件所期望的交互式、面向后端的接口。核心挑战在于：HPC托管的虚拟QPU必须在队列延迟、调度器分配、计算节点隔离和部分执行故障等条件下，保持拓扑、原生门和校准语义，同时不开放通往集群的入站路径。

Method: 提出HPC-vQPU服务导出架构，将面向云的控制平面与HPC驻留的执行平面分离。控制平面负责设备身份、任务生命周期、快照绑定和事件投影；执行平面认领工作并通过调度器支持的GPU作业实现。协调完全出站并由代理发起。核心抽象是拓扑和校准感知的设备快照，在认领时原子绑定并作为不可变契约带入执行，使每个调度作业封闭同时保持新鲜设备语义。

Result: 在Pawsey超级计算研究中心使用Setonix GPU、Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum和IBM Fez校准数据实现HPC-vQPU。生产实验表明：服务开销有界且可加，工作负载扩展限于模拟器；承载校准的快照产生可测量的输出偏移；认领时绑定防止了预认领设备变异后的陈旧执行；并发代理精确一次性完成50/50任务；显式恢复在代理故障后恢复陈旧运行任务。

Conclusion: 安全、调度器中介的HPC基础设施能够将设备保真的量子仿真正确导出为交互式虚拟QPU服务。

Abstract: Device-aware quantum simulation increasingly requires HPC-scale accelerators, yet secure supercomputers expose batch-scheduled execution environments rather than the interactive, backend-oriented interfaces expected by quantum software. The key obstacle is not only remote job submission: an HPC-hosted virtual QPU must preserve topology, native-gate, and calibration semantics across queue delay, scheduler allocation, compute-node isolation, and partial execution-side failures, without opening inbound paths into the cluster.
  We present HPC-vQPU, a service-export architecture for virtual QPUs on batch-scheduled HPC systems. HPC-vQPU separates a cloud-facing control plane, which owns device identity, task lifecycle, snapshot binding, and event projection, from an HPC-resident execution plane, which claims work and realises it through scheduler-backed GPU jobs. Coordination is exclusively outbound and agent initiated. The central abstraction is a topology- and calibration-aware device snapshot bound atomically at claim time and carried into execution as an immutable contract, making each scheduled job hermetic while preserving fresh device semantics.
  We implement HPC-vQPU at the Pawsey Supercomputing Research Centre using Setonix GPUs, Qiskit-Aer/cuQuantum, and IBM Fez calibration data. Production experiments show that service overhead is bounded and additive, while workload scaling remains confined to the simulator; calibration-bearing snapshots produce measurable output shifts; claim-time binding prevents stale execution after pre-claim device mutation; concurrent agents complete 50/50 tasks exactly once; and explicit recovery restores stale running tasks after agent failure. These results show that secure, scheduler-mediated HPC infrastructure can export device-faithful quantum simulation as an interactive virtual-QPU service.

</details>


### [158] [Understanding and Reducing Metadata-Driven Host Overheads in Sampling-Based GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29346)
*Yidong Gong,Saima Afrin,Yuchen Ma,Guannan Wang,Bin Ren,Pradeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZEROGNN系统，通过将元数据驱动的控制循环完全移至GPU端执行，消除了采样式GNN训练中CPU带来的主机-设备协调开销，实现了高达5.28倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载（特别是采样式图神经网络训练）呈现出动态、元数据驱动的执行特征，运行时生成的信息决定了内存分配和内核启动决策。这种特性使CPU成为关键路径，引入了持续的主机-设备协调开销和频繁的GPU-CPU同步，当GPU计算量较小时，这些开销主导了端到端运行时间。现有方法（包括CUDA Graphs和GPU动态并行性）无法解决此问题，因为元数据驱动的控制循环仍然依赖主机中介，且执行结构在迭代间变化。

Method: ZEROGNN系统将运行时元数据保留在设备端，在固定的启动结构中协调动态执行，并提供保守但紧凑的执行包络来恢复CUDA Graph的可重放性，从而将主机从元数据驱动的控制循环中移除，实现完全在GPU上驻留的执行。

Result: 实验结果表明，ZEROGNN在采样式GNN工作负载上实现了高达5.28倍的端到端加速，GPU执行分数接近100%，内存效率与理想的元数据感知分配相当，同时通过消除主机端瓶颈实现了强大的多GPU扩展性。

Conclusion: ZEROGNN成功解决了动态、元数据驱动工作负载中的主机-设备协调瓶颈问题，证明了通过设备端元数据管理和固定执行结构，可以实现高效的全GPU驻留执行，为动态深度学习工作负载的优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly exhibit dynamic, metadata-driven execution, where runtime-generated information determines memory provisioning and kernel launch decisions. In sampling-based graph neural network (GNN) training, this behavior places the CPU on the critical path, introducing persistent host-device orchestration overhead and frequent GPU-CPU synchronization, which dominate end-to-end runtime when GPU computation is small. Existing approaches, including CUDA Graphs and GPU dynamic parallelism, fail to address this problem because the metadata-driven control loop remains host-mediated, and execution structure varies across iterations. We present ZEROGNN, a system that removes the host from the metadata-driven control loop and enables fully GPU-resident execution under dynamic behavior. ZEROGNN keeps runtime metadata on-device, mediates dynamic execution within a fixed launch structure, and provisions a conservative yet tight execution envelope to restore CUDA Graph replayability. Experiments on sampling-based GNN workloads show that ZEROGNN achieves up to 5.28 x end-to-end speedup, near 100% GPU execution fraction, and memory efficiency comparable to ideal metadata-informed allocation, while enabling strong multi-GPU scaling by eliminating host-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [159] [Silent Data Corruption Protection through Efficient Task Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29506)
*Mia Reitz,Claudia Fohry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对异步多任务运行时中的静默数据损坏（SDC）问题，提出了一种新颖的复制方案，通过记录任务树并在结果不匹配时自顶向下遍历识别受影响任务并重新计算，在Itoyori运行时中实现了有效检测与纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级计算机集群规模不断增长导致程序越来越容易受到静默数据损坏（SDC）的影响，传统复制策略难以应用于异步多任务（AMT）运行时，因为动态任务生成和工作窃取机制使得识别复制任务变得复杂。

Method: 提出一种针对嵌套fork-join程序的复制方案：复制计算过程并记录任务树；当最终结果不匹配时，自顶向下遍历任务树识别所有可能影响最终结果的损坏任务；通过重新计算这些任务进行恢复，同时重用正确子任务的结果。在Itoyori集群AMT运行时变体中实现该方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，识别和重新处理受影响任务的时间开销可以忽略不计，证明了该方案的有效性和低开销特性。

Conclusion: 成功设计并实现了一种适用于异步多任务运行时的SDC检测与纠正方案，并通过实验验证了其高效性，同时讨论了该方案对未来协作任务的适应性。

Abstract: The trend of increasing cluster sizes of supercomputers leads to a growing susceptibility to Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that can invalidate program results. A common strategy for SDC protection is replication, where the computation is repeated, and the correct result is determined as the one that is the same in at least two different computations. Applying replication to Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes on clusters is challenging due to dynamic task spawning and work stealing, which complicate the identification of replicated tasks.
  To address the challenge, this paper introduces a novel replication scheme that detects and corrects SDCs for nested fork-join programs. Briefly stated, our approach replicates the computation and records the task tree. Upon a mismatch in the final result, it traverses the tree top-down to identify all corrupted tasks that could have impacted the final result. Recovery is then performed by recomputing these tasks, while the results of correct child tasks are reused.
  We demonstrate our implementation within a variant of the Itoyori cluster AMT runtime. Our experimental results suggest that the time to identify and reprocess the affected tasks is negligible. The paper concludes by discussing the adaptability of our scheme to tasks that cooperate through futures.

</details>


### [160] [Design and Implementation of a Serverless MapReduce Framework for Scalable Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29573)
*Angelos Dorotheos Chatzopoulos,Babis Andreou,Kakia Panagidi,Stathes Hadjiefthymiades*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个运行在Kubernetes上的事件驱动MapReduce框架，结合Knative和Apache Kafka实现物流环境的实时数据处理，支持基于负载的可配置扩展和按需处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物流系统从GPS、IoT传感器和物流管理系统等来源持续产生大量数据流，这些数据的聚合、处理和分析对于监控运营、优化效率和快速响应决策任务至关重要，因此需要一种能够实时处理这些数据的系统。

Method: 采用事件驱动的MapReduce框架，在Kubernetes平台上运行，使用Knative进行无服务器编排，Apache Kafka作为组件间通信骨干。系统由五个松耦合服务组成，负责实时接收、处理和聚合数据；使用Redis保存工作流元数据，AWS S3提供持久化存储；集成函数即服务（FaaS）原则与分布式处理技术，支持基于工作负载需求和底层硬件的可配置扩展。

Result: 实验评估表明，该系统在输入数据量增加时能够有效扩展，同时支持扩展至零和按需处理功能。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功实现了物流环境中的实时数据处理，具有良好的可扩展性和资源利用效率，能够根据工作负载动态调整处理能力。

Abstract: Modern logistics systems tend to generate continuous streams of data from sources such as GPS, IoT sensors, and logistics management systems. The aggregation, processing, and analysis of data have become vital for monitoring operations, optimizing efficiency, and responding quickly to decision making tasks. In this paper, an event-driven MapReduce framework for real-time data processing in logistics environments is presented. This system runs on Kubernetes with Knative and utilizes Apache Kafka as the backbone for communication between the components. This platform is composed of five loosely coupled services that receive, process, and aggregate the incoming data in real-time. Redis is used to preserve workflow metadata, while an AWS S3 service provides persistent storage for the framework. The design is inspired by the MapReduce programming model. It integrates Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) principles with distributed processing techniques that allow configurable scaling based on the workload demands and the underlying hardware. Experimental evaluation shows that the system can scale effectively as the input data volume increases while supporting scale-to-zero, on-demand processing.

</details>


### [161] [TC-MIS: Maximal Independent Set on Tensor-cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29604)
*Prajjwal Nijhara,Dip Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TC-MIS算法，利用GPU张量核心加速最大独立集(MIS)问题的求解，通过将MIS计算关键阶段重构为稀疏矩阵向量乘法(SpMV)，实现了相比现有方法最高44.38倍、平均18.80倍（H200 GPU）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大独立集(MIS)问题在资源分配、调度和网络优化中具有重要应用。传统GPU算法由于图的非结构化特性导致内存访问不规则和工作负载不平衡，性能受限。现代GPU配备的张量核心(TC)相比CUDA核心(CC)具有8-16倍更高的吞吐量，但其在图算法领域的应用尚未被充分探索，这为加速MIS算法提供了新的机遇。

Method: TC-MIS算法将MIS计算的关键阶段重构为稀疏矩阵向量乘法(SpMV)操作。该方法对图的邻接矩阵进行分块处理，采用Warp矩阵乘累加(WMMA)操作，将不规则的图遍历转换为规则的、大规模并行计算，从而充分利用张量核心的计算能力。

Result: 在支持TC的多种GPU微架构构上进行了评估：在RTX A5000上平均加速2.84倍，在L40S上加速4.84倍，在H200上加速18.80倍（最大加速44.38倍），在RTX 5080上加速5.20倍，同时保持了解决方案质量与现有启发式算法相当。

Conclusion: TC-MIS成功地将张量核心应用于图算法领域，证明了TC在传统图计算问题上的加速潜力。该方法在不牺牲解质量的前提下，通过矩阵操作重构实现了显著的性能提升，为GPU上图算法的设计提供了新的思路，特别是针对H200等高性能GPU架构展现了优异的可扩展性。

Abstract: Maximal Independent Set (MIS) in a graph is a fundamental problem with applications in resource allocation, scheduling, and network optimization. Although graphs are inherently un-structured and challenging for GPU parallelism due to irregular memory access and workload imbalance, specialized GPU algorithms have achieved good performance, processing million-vertex graphs in milliseconds. Modern GPUs are equipped with Tensor Cores (TCs), specialized units for matrix operations with 8-16x higher throughput than CUDA Cores (CCs), which are extensively used for ML, DL, and inference tasks but remain largely unexplored for graph algorithms. In this paper, we present TC-MIS, a TC-accelerated algorithm that reformulates key phases of MIS computation as sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV). TC-MIS tiles the graph adjacency matrix and employs Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) operations to transform irregular graph traversal into regular, massively parallel computation. Our evaluation across TC-enabled microarchitectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, Hopper, Blackwell) demonstrates that TC-MIS achieves an average speedup of 2.84x on RTX A5000, 4.84x on L40S, 18.80x on H200 GPUs, and 5.20x on RTX 5080 with a maximum speedup of 44.38x on H200 GPU over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining solution quality comparable to that obtained by established heuristics that produce near-maximum independent sets.

</details>


### [162] [AMDP: Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29664)
*Ling Chen,Houming Wu,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出异步多向流水线并行方法（AMDP），通过限制第一阶段处理批次数量、启动多个并发流水线以及跨批次梯度累积，在保持高利用率的同时解决了参数不匹配导致的收敛下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步流水线并行方法在前向和反向传播之间存在参数不匹配问题，导致收敛性能下降。

Method: AMDP限制每个流水线的第一阶段在反向传播前最多处理两个小批次，从而限制前向和反向传播间的参数更新次数；启动多个并发流水线并根据流水线深度调整数量以缓解流水线气泡；跨小批次累积梯度并单次更新，确保仅有有限数量的小批次经历参数不匹配。

Result: 在GPT和BERT风格模型上的实验表明，AMDP在保持收敛性的同时显著加速训练。

Conclusion: AMDP有效解决了异步流水线并行中的参数不匹配问题，实现了高利用率和收敛性保持的双重目标，为大规模模型训练提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for large-scale model training, but existing asynchronous approaches often degrade convergence due to parameter mismatch between forward and backward passes. We propose Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline parallelism (AMDP) to mitigate this issue while sustaining high utilization. AMDP limits the first stage of each pipeline to process at most two minibatches before backpropagation, bounding the number of parameter updates between forward and backward passes. To alleviate the resulting pipeline bubbles, AMDP launches multiple concurrent pipelines and adapts their number according to pipeline depth. In addition, AMDP accumulates gradients across minibatches and applies them in a single update, ensuring that only a bounded number of minibatches experience parameter mismatch, limited to within one optimization step. Experiments on GPT- and BERT-style models demonstrate that AMDP significantly accelerates training while preserving convergence.

</details>


### [163] [PRISM: Processing-In-Memory Sparse MTTKRP for Tensor Decomposition Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29728)
*Daniel Pacheco,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首次提出基于存内计算技术(PIM)的稀疏张量分解算法加速方案PRISM，通过优化spMTTKRP核心操作实现最高2.64倍加速，显著提升资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏张量分解算法CP-ALS中核心操作spMTTKRP具有强访存限制特性，在通用处理器上难以高效实现，亟需通过新架构突破内存墙瓶颈。

Method: 提出PRISM系统，采用UPMEM PIM架构实现spMTTKRP操作，通过系统分析不同分区策略、数据格式和内核优化，结合CPU异构协同计算加速稀疏张量分解。

Result: PIM方案和异构方案相比最先进CPU实现分别达到2.37倍和2.64倍加速；资源消耗效率（峰值性能使用率）显著优于CPU和GPU，但在某些工作负载下UPMEM分布式内存系统会限制性能表现。

Conclusion: 存内计算技术为稀疏张量分解算法提供有效加速方案，在资源效率方面具有优势，但分布式内存架构的工作负载适配性仍需改进。

Abstract: Sparse tensors are the most used representation of sparse multidimensional data. Operations that decompose them, selecting their most important features while reducing their dimension, have become prevalent procedures in machine learning. One of the most used tensor decomposition algorithms is the Alternating Least Squares Canonical Polyadic Decomposition (CP-ALS), where the most time-consuming operation is the Sparse Matricized Tensor Times Khatri-Rao Product (spMTTKRP). This operation is strongly memory-bound, making it hard to implement efficiently on general-purpose processors. This work proposes PRISM, the first approach to tackle this operation using Processing-In-Memory (PIM) technology. We extensively characterize different partitioning strategies, number formats, and kernel optimizations that efficiently adapt this operation to UPMEM PIM, which is further boosted by heterogeneous collaboration with the CPU. The experimental results show that the proposed PIM-based and heterogeneous approaches achieve up to 2.37x and 2.64x speedup compared to state-of-the-art CPU implementations, respectively. However, the UPMEM distributed memory system can significantly hinder performance on certain workloads. Nonetheless, the efficiency of resource consumption for this approach, measured by peak performance fraction usage, is significantly higher than for both CPU and GPU.

</details>


### [164] [CARM Tool: Cache-Aware Roofline Model Automatic Benchmarking and Application Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29740)
*José Morgado,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了自动化工具，为x86(Intel/AMD)、ARM和RISC-V等主流CPU架构构建Cache-aware Roofline Model(CARM)，支持跨架构的性能模型构建和深入的应用特征分析，实验偏差小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HPC系统和CPU架构日益复杂，应用开发和优化变得极具挑战性。现有的CARM支持工具存在明显缺陷：Intel Advisor仅限于特定厂商，ARM工具发展不足，而AMD和RISC-V则完全缺乏相应工具。这种跨架构工具的缺失严重限制了CARM优化指导在应用开发中的广泛应用。

Method: 开发了专门定制的汇编级微基准测试程序，覆盖现代CPU从标量运算到所有支持的向量ISA扩展的完整性能谱系，包括计算单元和所有内存层次结构。同时，利用性能计数器和动态二进制插桩技术，将应用分析集成到CARM框架中。该方法实现了跨x86(Intel/AMD)、ARM和RISC-V架构的自动化CARM支持。

Result: 通过所提出的自动化框架构建的CARM性能顶线在各种测试的架构最大值中偏差小于1%，证明了该方法的准确性和有效性。该工具成功填补了跨架构CARM支持工具的重要空白。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一个跨架构的自动化CARM构建工具，为HPC应用开发者提供了跨主流CPU架构的统一性能建模和优化指导能力，显著降低了架构复杂性带来的优化挑战。

Abstract: In recent years, HPC systems and CPU architectures as their central components, have become increasingly complex, making application development and optimization quite challenging. In this respect, intuitive performance models like the Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) offer effective guidance by providing insights into bottlenecks that limit the application's ability to reach the system's maximum performance. To fully exploit the benefits of CARM optimization guidance for application development, automatic tools for cross-architecture model construction and in-depth application characterization are absolutely essential. Given a plethora of existing CPU architectures, the current landscape of CARM-enabled tools covers either vendor-specific (Intel Advisor), not sufficiently developed (ARM) or simply non-existing (AMD, RISC-V) tools. This is a particular gap that this work intends to close by bringing automatic CARM support to all major CPU architectures and ISAs, i.e., x86 (Intel, AMD), ARM, and RISC-V, by developing assembly microbenchmarks specifically tailored to cover a full performance spectrum of modern CPUs (from scalar to all supported vector ISA extensions) for both computational units and all memory hierarchy levels. Additionally, this work integrates application analysis within the CARM framework using performance counters and dynamic binary instrumentation. Experimental results show that the CARM roofs constructed with the proposed automated framework provide less than a 1% deviation across various tested architectural maximums.

</details>


### [165] [Effective MPI: User-defined Datatypes and Cartesian Communicators for Zero-copy All-to-all Communication in Multidimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29970)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在任意d维环面拓扑上高效实现全对全通信的MPI算法，通过维度分解和零拷贝技术实现了与原生MPI_Alltoall相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意d维环面拓扑结构上实现高效的全对全通信操作，避免传统实现中昂贵的通信器分割开销和显式数据重排，同时为算法调优和适配不同高性能系统提供灵活性。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1)利用笛卡尔通信器将p进程分解为d个维度通信器并缓存以避免重复分割；(2)将全对全操作分解为d个维度上的MPI_Alltoall操作序列；(3)使用MPI派生数据类型实现隐式数据重排，采用双缓冲方案实现形式上的零拷贝；(4)通过选择p的因子分解和组件MPI_Alltoall的实现版本进行算法调优。

Result: 实验结果表明该算法与原生MPI_Alltoall实现具有竞争性甚至更优的性能，同时揭示了常见MPI_Alltoall实现中存在的问题。算法仅需要适度的临时缓冲区需求。

Conclusion: 该零拷贝全对全通信算法为d维环面拓扑提供了一种高效的MPI实现方案，具有充分的算法调优和系统适配空间，能够在特定场景下超越原生实现性能。

Abstract: We present and show how to implement a non-trivial all-to-all communication algorithm for arbitrary $d$-dimensional tori effectively in MPI. Given a factorization of the number of processes $p$ into $d$ factors that can be mapped onto a $d$-dimensional torus, we first utilize a Cartesian communicator to split a given $p$-process MPI communicator into, for each MPI process, $d$ smaller communicators spanning each of the dimensions of the torus to which the process belongs, and cache these communicators in order to avoid expensive splitting at each all-to-all operation. The all-to-all operation itself is decomposed into a sequence of $d$ MPI_Alltoall operations on the dimension-wise communicators. The non-trivial data rearrangement before and after each MPI_Alltoall call is implicit only and effected by MPI derived datatypes. This makes the implementation of the algorithm formally \emph{zero-copy}, meaning that no explicit process-local reordering of data blocks ever has to be performed. In order to achieve this, the algorithm employs a double-buffering scheme with modest temporary buffer requirements. By choosing the factorization of $p$ and selecting appropriate implementations for the component MPI_Alltoall operations, the presented implementation gives ample opportunities for algorithm tuning and adaptation to the particular high-performance system. A few, select experimental results show competitive performance with native MPI_Alltoall implementations and illustrate problems that common MPI_Alltoall implementations may have.

</details>


### [166] [RAFI -- A Ray/Work Forwarding Infrastructure for Data Parallel Multi-Node/Multi-GPU Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30294)
*Ingo Wald,Serkan Demirci,Alper Sahistan,Stefan Zellmann,Andrea Paris,Patrick Moran,Milan Jaros,Tatiana von Landesberger,Ugur Gudukbay,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RaFI是一个基于CUDA和MPI的软件框架，旨在简化构建支持GPU的数据并行软件的任务，这类软件中的光线或类似工作项需要在不同的GPU之间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在构建GPU加速的数据并行应用程序时，光线追踪等需要跨GPU迁移工作项的任务往往面临复杂的通信管理挑战。现有的CUDA和MPI通信机制需要开发者手动处理大量底层细节，这增加了软件开发难度和维护成本，因此需要一个能够抽象和自动化这些通信过程的框架。

Method: RaFI提供了一个简化的接口，允许CUDA内核直接将工作项转发到其他GPU。框架在底层自动管理所有CUDA和MPI相关的通信工作，包括消息打包、传输、解包和调度等复杂操作，开发者无需关心具体的通信实现细节。

Result: 通过多个示例应用程序的演示，RaFI成功展示了其在处理GPU间工作项迁移方面的能力。框架有效地简化了并行软件开发流程，证明了其在实际应用中的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: RaFI框架有效地解决了GPU数据并行软件中工作项迁移的复杂性难题，通过抽象通信细节显著降低了开发难度，为构建需要跨GPU通信的应用程序提供了一个实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present RaFI, a CUDA and MPI based software framework that simplifies the task of building GPU-enabled data-parallel software where rays or similar work items need to migrate between different GPUs. RaFI provides a simple interface for CUDA kernels to forward such work items to other GPUs, while under the hood managing all the CUDA and MPI related work required to make this happen. We describe RaFI's motivation and implementation, and show its potential in several example applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法通过修改MLP权重更新事实关联，但内部机制未被充分探索。本研究发现尽管事实特定的权重变化不同，这些编辑方法靶向相同的权重子集。通过训练紧凑的二进制掩码，成功逆转了80%的训练集编辑和超过70%的测试集编辑，揭示了编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识编辑方法（如ROME和MEMIT）主要通过输出行为进行评估，其内部机制尚未被充分探索。研究旨在探究不同事实的编辑是否依赖于共同机制，以及这些编辑方法的具体工作原理。

Method: 训练一个紧凑的二进制掩码来隔离关键权重子集，分析掩码如何逆转编辑效果。通过研究掩码在不同层的作用，分析其对编辑成功率和相关事实传播的影响。

Result: 掩码能够逆转80%的训练集编辑和超过70%的测试集编辑，确认不同编辑共享共同的功能结构。掩码通过消除后续层的过度注意力来逆转编辑。在编辑过程中注入掩码可将编辑成功率从98%降至38%。编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识来工作，这解释了为何ROME和MEMIT无法将变化传播到相关事实。

Conclusion: 研究发现知识编辑靶向一个共同的功能子空间，编辑机制是通过抑制而非覆盖知识。这一发现解释了现有方法的局限性，并为检测和防御不良编辑提供了理论基础。识别出的共同功能子空间可以用于改进知识编辑方法的安全性。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [168] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用TradeArena测试平台研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动力学，发现失败前存在可测量的嵌入漂移、表征分离与有效秩收缩等预警信号，并揭示了对齐盲点与风险反馈的双重作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：金融决策环境高风险且复杂，LLM作为代理在市场压力下如何演进、何时出现推理失败或对齐漂移尚不明确；需要可审计的测试平台来分析其规划、风险、行为随时间演化的内部表征，从而诊断潜在失效、提升安全性与可解释性。

Method: 构建TradeArena可审计交易代理测试平台，具备风险报告、执行模拟、记忆与可回放轨迹；采用规划嵌入、融合规划-风险表征、流形诊断（如有效秩收缩）等方法监测状态漂移；在8条LLM轨迹上使用80个滚动失效锚点，跨多种表征（哈希、LSA、Transformer、白盒隐状态）验证收缩现象；设计消融与对照（去链式思考、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计）以及带结构化风险反馈的干预；在51只股票的日内实验中，以滚动Markowitz基线为协方差参考，分析LLM理由对耦合资产集中暴露的风险层裁剪情况。

Result: 测量到失效前预兆：正常态质心的规划嵌入漂移、融合表征可分离正常与回撤前状态、流形诊断显示有效秩在失效前收缩；跨多种嵌入与表征探针，该收缩现象稳健；消融显示：若无理由生成，理由层面收缩消失但意图空间收缩可能保留，词汇多样性未坍缩，融合签名在噪声下仍有信息量；结构化风险反馈作为外部对齐信号有效但非通用性能提升器：真实审计改善部分模型的校准或收益回撤，虚假或隐藏反馈短期回报可能更高但对齐诊断更弱；日内实验揭示相关盲点：LLM理由常为对耦合资产的高集中暴露辩护，风险层反复裁剪，体现对齐偏移。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈与表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时正在对齐、漂移或失败；该结果支持研究主张而非盈利主张，为LLM在金融场景的安全性与可解释性提供方法与证据。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [169] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性层面研究了强化学习(RL)相比监督微调(SFT)更能缓解灾难性遗忘的原因，发现RL能更好地保留基础模型的内部计算电路，但以适应速度为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明强化学习(RL)在缓解灾难性遗忘方面优于监督微调(SFT)，但其机制层面的原因尚不清楚。本文旨在探究RL的优势是否源于更好地保留了模型内部的计算电路。

Method: 提出"差分电路脆弱性"(differential circuit vulnerability)这一注意力头级别的度量指标，用于量化电路在微调过程中的退化程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上，将其适配到科学问答任务，对比分析RL和SFT两种微调方式对内部电路的影响。

Result: 发现明显的机制权衡：SFT能更快适应目标任务，但会导致更严重的电路破坏和先前能力的遗忘；而RL保留了更大比例的基础电路，但任务适应速度较慢。这种电路保留程度的差异可以解释为何RL对灾难性遗忘更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 电路保留机制有助于解释强化学习微调方法在缓解灾难性遗忘方面的优势，为理解不同微调范式对模型内部结构的影响提供了机制层面的见解。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [170] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACE，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的轨迹感知分子先导优化智能体，将工具选择建模为动作轨迹上的序列决策问题，在保持分子相似性的同时，显著提升了ADMET优化任务的成功率、性质改善幅度与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现过程漫长且资源密集，其中先导优化阶段至关重要。现有AI方法多采用单步分子优化，忽视了序列设计决策的长期影响，无法在结构约束下进行前瞻性优化。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，将工具选择构建为动作轨迹上的序列决策问题。给定先导分子和优化目标，该智能体在分子优化工具上进行轨迹感知决策，实现在结构约束下的前瞻性优化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务上的实验表明，与基线模型相比，TRACE在保持分子相似度的同时，实现了更高的优化成功率、更大的性质改善幅度以及更高的有效性。

Conclusion: TRACE通过轨迹感知的序列决策机制，有效解决了先导优化中的长期规划问题，为药物发现中的分子优化提供了一种更高效、更可靠的AI解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [171] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了针对四人非完美信息扑克游戏“大老二”的自博弈强化学习框架，系统比较了多种算法，发现PPO在该任务上最优，并揭示了熵正则化与自博弈策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完美信息多人博弈对智能体的决策能力提出了隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手等严峻挑战。作者希望在一个可控的、具有代表性的多玩家非完美信息游戏环境中，系统评估和比较不同深度强化学习算法的性能，以推动该领域的研究进展。

Method: 1. 选择四人非完美信息卡牌游戏“大老二”作为研究环境。
2. 开发了一个统一的自博弈强化学习框架，确保不同算法在相同的环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议下进行比较。
3. 在此框架下对比了PPO（策略梯度类）、蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q学习（价值近似类）等算法。
4. 进一步研究了熵正则化强度以及不同的自博弈训练模式（当前策略自博弈、检查点自博弈、固定对手训练）对智能体性能的影响。

Result: 1. 在统一实验条件下，PPO算法的表现优于蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q学习算法，对抗随机、贪心和启发式对手时胜率更高。
2. 适度的熵正则化能够防止策略过早收敛至确定性策略，从而提升PPO的性能。
3. 与检查点自博弈或固定对手训练相比，基于当前策略的自博弈在有限训练预算下能提供更优的课程学习效果。

Conclusion: 研究证明了“大老二”是研究非完美信息、多玩家交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下深度强化学习的一个有用且可控的测试平台，并为该类任务中算法选择与训练策略设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [172] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，世界模型通过物理探索自发学习到物理世界的几何结构作为其潜在空间的组织原则。预测性能与语义对齐共同变化，证实了几何结构是核心驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有任何语言监督的情况下，世界模型仅通过物理探索能学习到什么。研究者假设答案由单一原则组织：物理世界的几何结构。

Method: 在随机具身探索数据上训练VAE-based世界模型；分析潜在空间是否发展出反映物理几何的空间语义结构；进行双敲除实验(double knockout)，操纵KL正则化系数beta(0.1 vs 0.001)，观察预测性能和语义对齐的共同变化。

Result: 训练后的模型潜在空间展现出明确的几何结构：方向准确率0.677±0.029（随机编码器0.547）；位置RSA 0.192±0.047（随机编码器0.029，提升6.6倍）。预测性能与语义对齐共同改善(Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004)。高KL正则化(beta=0.1)导致两种能力同时崩溃至接近机会水平；降低beta至0.001则同时恢复两种能力。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则。这一发现对设计语义基础化的具身智能体具有直接启示，表明几何结构可能是实现语义落地的基础性机制。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [173] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COM策略，通过在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束来保持时间序列token的连续性和有序性，从而提升基于token的时间序列大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型（TS-LLMs）忽略了时间序列token固有的连续性和有序性，这两项属性对于时间序列分析至关重要，其缺失严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，该策略同时考虑连续性和有序性，将几何约束集成到模型初始化阶段和训练阶段中，确保时间序列token嵌入能够保持这些关键属性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准测试上的实验结果表明，COM策略能够持续提升基于token的TS-LLMs的性能，取得了具有竞争力的结果，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在基于token的时间序列大语言模型中，保持时间序列token的连续性和有序性对于模型效果至关重要，COM策略通过几何约束有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [174] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PrismFlow，一种结合Koopman动态专家和置信度感知WTA目标的Flow Matching方法，有效解决了时间序列生成中的频谱收缩问题，实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列数据通常表现出多模态模式和多重尺度动态（如振荡和高频变化），而现有的Flow Matching方法依赖单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器。在这种异质时间分布中，不同状态可能经过相似的流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度，导致单一估计器学习到过度平滑的局部传输场近似，从而引起频谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow方法，包含两个核心组件：（1）受Koopman启发的动态专家，每个专家在潜在空间中学习残差修正，其中局部非线性时间演化可以通过线性转换近似；（2）置信度感知的Winner-Take-All（WTA）目标函数，仅更新与每个样本最匹配的专家，同时屏蔽其他专家的梯度，鼓励特定模式的专门化。在采样过程中，选定的专家向全局传输场添加残差动态修正。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准Flow Matching中的频谱收缩问题，实现了最先进的性能：Context-FID提升15.6%，判别分数提高38.6%；同时在低数据环境下保持鲁棒性，并在预测和填补任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过动态专家机制成功解决了Flow Matching在异质时间分布中的过度平滑问题，在保持流匹配稳定性的同时恢复了细粒度和高频时间结构，为时间序列生成提供了一个有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [175] [TaxDistill: Improving Metagenomic Taxonomic Annotation via Distilled Genomic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28868)
*Rongye Ye,Lun Li,Zheng Luo,Yiran Zhan,Shuhui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TaxDistill知识蒸馏框架，利用500M参数的基因组基础模型GenomeOcean作为教师网络生成软标签，有效降低了元基因组分类中初始检索工具引入的标签噪声，在CAMI2数据集上显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的元基因组分类方法依赖序列相似性搜索，受限于微生物多样性和参考数据库不完整性。现有的学习方法（如Taxometer）在训练时使用相似性搜索工具生成的标签，不可避免地引入噪声，损害表示学习和分类性能。

Method: 提出TaxDistill知识蒸馏框架，引入500M参数的基因组基础模型GenomeOcean作为教师网络，提取深度语义特征并基于置信度生成软标签。通过将软标签信息蒸馏到轻量级学生网络中，有效减少初始检索工具引入的标签噪声。

Result: 在7个多样化的CAMI2数据集上进行了全面实验，TaxDistill在大多数场景下优于现有基线。例如在胃肠道数据集上，将MMseqs2的F1分数从0.763提升至0.941，超越了Taxometer基线。

Conclusion: TaxDistill为复杂元基因组分析中的标签校正提供了可靠方法，有效解决了传统方法中标签噪声问题，提升了分类准确性。

Abstract: Metagenomic taxonomic annotation aims to identify the microbial origins of DNA fragments in environmental samples. Traditional methods that rely on sequence similarity are often constrained by the high microbial diversity and the incompleteness of reference databases, which has motivated the development of learning approaches such as Taxometer that perform post hoc correction to learn more informative metagenomic sequence representations. However, these methods typically rely on labels derived from similarity search tools during training, which inevitably introduces noise that can impair representation learning and degrade classification performance. To address this issue, we propose TaxDistill, a knowledge distillation framework for metagenomic classification. We introduce GenomeOcean, a 500M parameter genomic foundation model, as the teacher network to extract deep semantic features and generate soft labels based on confidence. By distilling this soft label information into a lightweight student network, TaxDistill effectively reduces the label noise introduced by initial retrieval tools. Comprehensive experiments on seven diverse CAMI2 datasets demonstrate that TaxDistill outperforms existing baselines in most scenarios. For instance, on the Gastrointestinal dataset, it improves the F1 score of MMseqs2 from 0.763 to 0.941, outperforming the Taxometer baseline. Overall, TaxDistill provides a reliable method for label correction in complex metagenomic analysis.

</details>


### [176] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中的模态不平衡问题，提出首个从标签侧设计的方法BMLR，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来平衡不同模态的映射难度，有效促进了模态交互并提升了整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中常存在模态不平衡问题，收敛较快的模态会主导优化过程，而其他模态则训练不足。现有方法通过增强弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解该问题，但主要是在补偿优化速率差异，往往以牺牲强模态的优化能力为代价，且未从模态层面分析这些差异的产生原因。作者基于理论洞察和实证观察，认为学习节奏的差异源于模态特定特征空间与共享标签空间之间映射难度的不同。

Method: 提出平衡多模态标签重塑方法（BMLR），这是首个从标签侧设计促进多模态平衡的方法。BMLR通过重塑跨模态标签空间来平衡各模态间的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并为每个模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR能够持续改善多模态性能，并与多种模型设计展现出强大的兼容性。

Conclusion: BMLR通过从标签侧重塑跨模态标签空间，有效解决了多模态学习中因映射难度差异导致的模态不平衡问题，为多模态平衡提供了一种新的解决思路，且具有良好的通用性和兼容性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [177] [FedQHD: Closed-Form Function-Space Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29002)
*Yuchen Hou,Yongshan Chen,Zhuowen Zou,Calvin Yeung,Mohsen Imani,Tian Lan,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedQHD方法，通过使用超维随机特征编码器和线性读出结构，解决了联邦强化学习中FedAvg式参数平均在函数空间不一致的问题，实现了异构编码器下的高效聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习中的FedAvg式参数平均存在函数空间不一致问题：当客户端使用异构编码器或非线性网络时，平均参数无法对应任何公共函数空间中客户端价值函数的加权平均，导致聚合效果不佳。

Method: 提出FedQHD方法：采用超维（随机特征）状态编码器配合线性读出，使Q函数对状态非线性但对可训练参数线性。对于共享编码器，函数空间共识更新等同于局部读出矩阵的加权平均；对于异构编码器，服务器通过在共享锚点状态集上平均客户端Q值构建全局教师，客户端通过单次岭回归投影将教师编译到其局部表示中。

Result: 在四个连续状态离散动作控制基准测试中，FedQHD匹配或优于FedAvg基线和基于蒸馏的替代方法，同时需要更少的计算量。经验上观察到联邦差距对编码器维度的依赖与理论分析一致，验证了锚点-维度比m≥D_i作为良好条件区域的理论预测。

Conclusion: 形式化了联邦差距概念，将其分解为子空间不对齐、锚点集条件和正则化偏差三个组成部分，识别出锚点-维度比m≥D_i作为良好条件区域，在该区域联邦差距降低为编码器异构性地板的倍数。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning enables decentralized agents to collaboratively improve policies or value estimates without exchanging raw trajectories. However, FedAvg-style parameter averaging is not function-space consistent: when clients use heterogeneous encoders or even identical nonlinear networks, averaged parameters need not correspond to the weighted average of client value functions in any common function space. We propose FedQHD, a federated Q-learning method using hyperdimensional (random-feature) state encoders with a linear readout, so that Q-functions are nonlinear in state yet linear in trainable parameters. This linear structure enables closed-form aggregation. With a shared encoder, the function-space consensus update coincides exactly with weighted averaging of local readout matrices. With heterogeneous encoders, the server constructs a global teacher by averaging client Q-values on a shared anchor-state set, and each client compiles this teacher into its local representation via a single ridge projection. We formalize the federation gap -- the error incurred when compiling a federated teacher into a heterogeneous client representation -- relative to a client-specific oracle projection. We show that this gap decomposes into subspace misalignment, anchor-set conditioning, and regularization bias. We further identify the anchor-to-dimension ratio $m \geq D_i$ as the well-conditioned regime in which the gap reduces to a multiple of the encoder heterogeneity floor. On four continuous-state, discrete-action control benchmarks, FedQHD matches or outperforms FedAvg-style baselines and distillation-based alternatives while requiring substantially less computation, and the empirical dependence of the federation gap on encoder dimension matches our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [178] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过“信号-偏差-噪声”三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表征假设，发现线性表征假设能解释跨模态对齐，中心化和归一化可缓解模型偏差，数据稀缺导致的噪声影响对齐，并据此提出统计模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入研究柏拉图表征假设（PRH），理解不同AI模型表征为何趋同，通过分解表征为信号、偏差和噪声三要素，系统性地探究表征对齐的内在机制。

Method: 提出三部分框架：1) 信号：利用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征，验证线性表征假设；2) 偏差：通过中心化和归一化缓解不同架构带来的隐式偏差；3) 噪声：分析词频与对齐强度的相关性，揭示数据稀缺导致的表征噪声。最后综合三要素提出统计模型。

Result: 1) 稀疏表征比稠密表征展现出更强的跨模态对齐；2) 中心化和归一化一致性地改善了跨模型对齐；3) 发现词频与LLM及文本嵌入模型的对齐强度之间存在强正相关；4) 提出的统计模型成功改进了线性表征假设，解释了多样化AI架构表征对齐现象。

Conclusion: 通过信号、偏差、噪声的三元框架，成功解释了柏拉图表征假设的内在机制，证明了线性表征假设的有效性，提出了可操作的偏差缓解方法，并建立了更完善的统计模型来理解现代AI表征对齐现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [179] [Q-ANCHOR: Federated Quantum Learning with ZNE-guided Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30075)
*Hoang M. Ngo,Quan Nguyen,Wanli Xing,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-ANCHOR框架，通过零噪声外推和有状态客户端校正，有效解决量子联邦学习中由非IID数据和量子硬件噪声导致的双重漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子联邦学习普遍采用FedAvg聚合方法，但在实际量子硬件部署时会遭遇严重的双重漂移现象：全局模型同时受到非IID数据导致的客户端漂移和量子硬件噪声梯度估计产生的硬件偏差影响，而标准平均方法无法消除硬件偏差造成的持续误差底限。

Method: 提出Q-ANCHOR量子感知联邦聚合架构，核心包含两个机制：(1)在服务器端使用零噪声外推技术锚定更新；(2)应用有状态客户端校正机制，同时抑制客户端漂移和硬件诱导偏差。

Result: 收敛性理论证明Q-ANCHOR成功缓解了经典客户端漂移，同时主动降低了硬件偏差底限；实验结果表明Q-ANCHOR相比传统联邦学习基线实现了显著更稳定的训练过程。

Conclusion: Q-ANCHOR为在噪声量子硬件上部署实用的量子联邦学习系统提供了有效的解决方案，从理论和实验双重角度验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Quantum Federated Learning (QFL) offers a promising framework to train quantum models across distributed clients while keeping data strictly local. Due to its simplicity and low communication overhead, Federated Averaging (FedAvg) is the standard aggregation choice in QFL literature. However, deploying QFL on practical hardware exposes a severe double-drift phenomenon: the global model is simultaneously derailed by client drift from non-IID data and hardware bias from noisy quantum gradient estimates. In this work, we first analyze the convergence of FedAvg under these realistic conditions, mathematically demonstrating that quantum hardware bias creates a persistent error floor that standard averaging cannot correct. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-ANCHOR, a quantum-aware federated aggregation architecture that anchors server updates with zero-noise extrapolation while applying stateful client correction to suppress both client drift and hardware-induced bias. Our convergence theory proves that Q-ANCHOR successfully mitigates classical client drift while actively reducing the hardware-bias floor. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-ANCHOR achieves significantly more stable training than conventional FL baselines.

</details>
